```
1→"""Zone classification system — deterministic file intent classification.
     2→
     3→Classifies files into zones (production, test, config, generated, script, vendor)
     4→based on path patterns. Zone metadata flows through issues, scoring, and the LLM.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import logging
    10→import os
    11→from collections.abc import Callable
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    13→from enum import Enum
    14→
    15→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    16→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones_data import (
    17→    CONFIG_SKIP_DETECTORS,
    18→    SCRIPT_SKIP_DETECTORS,
    19→    SKIP_ALL_DETECTORS,
    20→    TEST_SKIP_DETECTORS,
    21→)
    22→
    23→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    24→
    25→
    26→class Zone(str, Enum):
    27→    """File intent zone — determines scoring and detection policy."""
    28→
    29→    PRODUCTION = "production"
    30→    TEST = "test"
    31→    CONFIG = "config"
    32→    GENERATED = "generated"
    33→    SCRIPT = "script"
    34→    VENDOR = "vendor"
    35→
    36→
    37→# Zones excluded from the health score
    38→EXCLUDED_ZONES = {Zone.TEST, Zone.CONFIG, Zone.GENERATED, Zone.VENDOR}
    39→
    40→# String values for quick lookup in scoring (issues store zone as string)
    41→EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES = {z.value for z in EXCLUDED_ZONES}
    42→
    43→# Shared review filtering sets (enum-backed to keep comparisons type-safe).
    44→REVIEW_SELECTION_EXCLUDED_ZONES = frozenset({Zone.TEST, Zone.GENERATED, Zone.VENDOR})
    45→REVIEW_COVERAGE_EXCLUDED_ZONES = frozenset(
    46→    {Zone.TEST, Zone.GENERATED, Zone.VENDOR, Zone.CONFIG, Zone.SCRIPT}
    47→)
    48→
    49→
    50→def normalize_zone(zone: object) -> Zone | None:
    51→    """Normalize enum/string/duck-typed zone objects to a ``Zone`` member."""
    52→    if isinstance(zone, Zone):
    53→        return zone
    54→    raw = getattr(zone, "value", zone)
    55→    if isinstance(raw, Zone):
    56→        return raw
    57→    if isinstance(raw, str):
    58→        try:
    59→            return Zone(raw)
    60→        except ValueError:
    61→            return None
    62→    return None
    63→
    64→
    65→def zone_in(zone: object, zones: frozenset[Zone] | set[Zone]) -> bool:
    66→    """Return ``True`` when *zone* resolves to a member of *zones*."""
    67→    normalized = normalize_zone(zone)
    68→    return normalized in zones if normalized is not None else False
    69→
    70→
    71→@dataclass
    72→class ZoneRule:
    73→    """A classification rule: zone + list of path patterns.
    74→
    75→    Patterns are matched against relative file paths. First matching rule wins.
    76→    Pattern types (auto-detected from shape):
    77→      - "/dir/"   → substring match on full path (directory marker)
    78→      - ".ext"    → basename ends-with (suffix/extension, e.g. ".d.ts", ".test.")
    79→      - "prefix_" → basename starts-with (trailing underscore)
    80→      - "name.py" → basename exact match (has extension, no /)
    81→      - fallback  → substring on full path
    82→    """
    83→
    84→    zone: Zone
    85→    patterns: list[str]
    86→
    87→
    88→def _match_pattern(rel_path: str, pattern: str) -> bool:
    89→    """Match a zone pattern against a relative file path.
    90→
    91→    See ZoneRule docstring for pattern type conventions.
    92→    """
    93→    basename = os.path.basename(rel_path)
    94→
    95→    # Directory pattern: "/dir/" → substring on padded path
    96→    if pattern.startswith("/") and pattern.endswith("/"):
    97→        return pattern in ("/" + rel_path + "/")
    98→
    99→    # Suffix/extension pattern: starts with "." → contains on basename
   100→    if pattern.startswith("."):
   101→        return pattern in basename
   102→
   103→    # Prefix pattern: ends with "_" → basename starts-with
   104→    if pattern.endswith("_"):
   105→        return basename.startswith(pattern)
   106→
   107→    # Suffix pattern: starts with "_" → basename ends-with (_test.py, _pb2.py)
   108→    if pattern.startswith("_"):
   109→        return basename.endswith(pattern)
   110→
   111→    # Exact basename: has a proper file extension (1-5 chars after last dot),
   112→    # no "/" → exact basename match (config.py, setup.py, conftest.py)
   113→    if "/" not in pattern and "." in pattern:
   114→        ext = pattern.rsplit(".", 1)[-1]
   115→        if ext and len(ext) <= 5 and ext.isalnum():
   116→            return basename == pattern
   117→
   118→    # Fallback: substring on full path (vite.config, tsconfig, eslint, etc.)
   119→    return pattern in rel_path
   120→
   121→
   122→# ── Common zone rules (shared across languages) ──────────
   123→
   124→COMMON_ZONE_RULES = [
   125→    ZoneRule(Zone.VENDOR, ["/vendor/", "/third_party/", "/vendored/"]),
   126→    ZoneRule(Zone.GENERATED, ["/generated/", "/__generated__/"]),
   127→    ZoneRule(Zone.TEST, ["/tests/", "/test/", "/fixtures/"]),
   128→    ZoneRule(Zone.SCRIPT, ["/scripts/", "/bin/"]),
   129→]
   130→
   131→
   132→def classify_file(
   133→    rel_path: str, rules: list[ZoneRule], overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
   134→) -> Zone:
   135→    """Classify a file by its relative path. Overrides take priority."""
   136→    if overrides:
   137→        override = overrides.get(rel_path)
   138→        if override:
   139→            try:
   140→                return Zone(override)
   141→            except ValueError as exc:
   142→                log_best_effort_failure(
   143→                    logger, f"parse zone override for {rel_path}", exc
   144→                )
   145→    for rule in rules:
   146→        for pattern in rule.patterns:
   147→            if _match_pattern(rel_path, pattern):
   148→                return rule.zone
   149→    return Zone.PRODUCTION
   150→
   151→
   152→class FileZoneMap:
   153→    """Cached zone classification for a set of files.
   154→
   155→    Built once per scan from file list + zone rules.
   156→    """
   157→
   158→    def __init__(
   159→        self,
   160→        files: list[str],
   161→        rules: list[ZoneRule],
   162→        rel_fn: Callable[[str], str] | None = None,
   163→        overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   164→    ):
   165→        """Build a zone map from files, ordered rules, and optional overrides."""
   166→        self._map: dict[str, Zone] = {}
   167→        self._rel_map: dict[str, Zone] = {}
   168→        self._rel_fn = rel_fn
   169→        self._overrides = overrides
   170→        for file_path in files:
   171→            rel_path = rel_fn(file_path) if rel_fn else file_path
   172→            zone = classify_file(rel_path, rules, overrides)
   173→            self._map[file_path] = zone
   174→            self._rel_map[rel_path] = zone
   175→
   176→    def get(self, path: str) -> Zone:
   177→        """Get zone for a file path. Returns PRODUCTION if not classified."""
   178→        direct = self._map.get(path)
   179→        if direct is not None:
   180→            return direct
   181→
   182→        rel_direct = self._rel_map.get(path)
   183→        if rel_direct is not None:
   184→            return rel_direct
   185→
   186→        if self._rel_fn is not None:
   187→            try:
   188→                rel_path = self._rel_fn(path)
   189→            except (OSError, TypeError, ValueError):
   190→                rel_path = path
   191→            rel_zone = self._rel_map.get(rel_path)
   192→            if rel_zone is not None:
   193→                return rel_zone
   194→
   195→        return Zone.PRODUCTION
   196→
   197→    def exclude(self, files: list[str], *zones: Zone) -> list[str]:
   198→        """Return files NOT in the given zones."""
   199→        zone_set = set(zones)
   200→        return [
   201→            file_path
   202→            for file_path in files
   203→            if self._map.get(file_path, Zone.PRODUCTION) not in zone_set
   204→        ]
   205→
   206→    def include_only(self, files: list[str], *zones: Zone) -> list[str]:
   207→        """Return files that ARE in the given zones."""
   208→        zone_set = set(zones)
   209→        return [
   210→            file_path
   211→            for file_path in files
   212→            if self._map.get(file_path, Zone.PRODUCTION) in zone_set
   213→        ]
   214→
   215→    def counts(self) -> dict[str, int]:
   216→        """Return file count per zone."""
   217→        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   218→        for zone in self._map.values():
   219→            counts[zone.value] = counts.get(zone.value, 0) + 1
   220→        return counts
   221→
   222→    def production_count(self) -> int:
   223→        """Count files classified as production."""
   224→        return len(self._map) - self.non_production_count()
   225→
   226→    def non_production_count(self) -> int:
   227→        """Count files in excluded zones (test/config/generated/vendor)."""
   228→        return sum(1 for z in self._map.values() if z in EXCLUDED_ZONES)
   229→
   230→    def all_files(self) -> list[str]:
   231→        """Return all classified file paths."""
   232→        return list(self._map.keys())
   233→
   234→    def items(self) -> list[tuple[str, Zone]]:
   235→        """Return all (path, zone) pairs."""
   236→        return list(self._map.items())
   237→
   238→
   239→# ── Zone detection policies ────────────────────────────────
   240→
   241→
   242→@dataclass
   243→class ZonePolicy:
   244→    """Per-zone detection policy.
   245→
   246→    skip_detectors: detectors to skip entirely for this zone.
   247→    downgrade_detectors: detectors whose confidence is downgraded to "low".
   248→    exclude_from_score: whether issues in this zone are excluded from scoring.
   249→    """
   250→
   251→    skip_detectors: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
   252→    downgrade_detectors: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
   253→    exclude_from_score: bool = False
   254→
   255→
   256→ZONE_POLICIES: dict[Zone, ZonePolicy] = {
   257→    Zone.PRODUCTION: ZonePolicy(),
   258→    Zone.TEST: ZonePolicy(
   259→        skip_detectors=TEST_SKIP_DETECTORS,
   260→        downgrade_detectors={"smells", "structural"},
   261→        exclude_from_score=True,
   262→    ),
   263→    Zone.CONFIG: ZonePolicy(
   264→        skip_detectors=CONFIG_SKIP_DETECTORS,
   265→        exclude_from_score=True,
   266→    ),
   267→    Zone.GENERATED: ZonePolicy(
   268→        skip_detectors=SKIP_ALL_DETECTORS,
   269→        exclude_from_score=True,
   270→    ),
   271→    Zone.VENDOR: ZonePolicy(
   272→        skip_detectors=SKIP_ALL_DETECTORS,
   273→        exclude_from_score=True,
   274→    ),
   275→    Zone.SCRIPT: ZonePolicy(
   276→        skip_detectors=SCRIPT_SKIP_DETECTORS,
   277→        downgrade_detectors={"structural"},
   278→    ),
   279→}
   280→
   281→
   282→# ── Helpers for phase runners ─────────────────────────────
   283→
   284→
   285→def adjust_potential(zone_map, total: int) -> int:
   286→    """Subtract non-production files from a potential count.
   287→
   288→    Uses the zone map's own file list — no need to pass files separately.
   289→    No-op if zone_map is None.
   290→    """
   291→    if zone_map is None:
   292→        return total
   293→    return max(total - zone_map.non_production_count(), 0)
   294→
   295→
   296→def should_skip_issue(zone_map, filepath: str, detector: str) -> bool:
   297→    """Check if a issue should be skipped based on zone policy.
   298→
   299→    Returns True if the file's zone policy says to skip this detector.
   300→    """
   301→    if zone_map is None:
   302→        return False
   303→    zone = zone_map.get(filepath)
   304→    policy = ZONE_POLICIES.get(zone)
   305→    return policy is not None and detector in policy.skip_detectors
   306→
   307→
   308→def filter_entries(
   309→    zone_map, entries: list[dict], detector: str, file_key: str = "file"
   310→) -> list[dict]:
   311→    """Filter detector entries by zone policy. No-op if zone_map is None.
   312→
   313→    If file_key points to a list (e.g. cycle entries with "files"), checks
   314→    the first element.
   315→    """
   316→    if zone_map is None:
   317→        return entries
   318→
   319→    def _get_path(entry):
   320→        val = entry[file_key]
   321→        return val[0] if isinstance(val, list) else val
   322→
   323→    return [
   324→        e for e in entries if not should_skip_issue(zone_map, _get_path(e), detector)
   325→    ]
   326→
```

```
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass

     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    12→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import is_subjective_phase
    13→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZONE_POLICIES, FileZoneMap
    14→from desloppify.languages.framework import (
    15→    DetectorPhase,
    16→    LangConfig,
    17→    LangRun,
    18→    auto_detect_lang,
    19→    available_langs,
    20→    capability_report,
    21→    get_lang,
    22→    make_lang_run,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.state_io import Issue
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class PlanScanOptions:
    29→    """Config object for scan execution behavior."""
    30→
    31→    include_slow: bool = True
    32→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
    33→    profile: str = "full"
    34→
    35→
    36→def _stderr(msg: str) -> None:
    37→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    38→
    39→
    40→def _resolve_lang(
    41→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None, project_root: Path
    42→) -> LangConfig | LangRun:
    43→    if lang is not None:
    44→        return lang
    45→
    46→    detected = auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    47→    if detected is None:
    48→        langs = available_langs()
    49→        if not langs:
    50→            raise ValueError("No language plugins available")
    51→        detected = langs[0]
    52→    return get_lang(detected)
    53→
    54→
    55→def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
    56→    if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
    57→        return
    58→
    59→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    60→    lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    61→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
    62→    )
    63→    counts = lang.zone_map.counts()
    64→    zone_str = ", ".join(
    65→        f"{zone}: {count}" for zone, count in sorted(counts.items()) if count > 0
    66→    )
    67→    _stderr(f"  Zones: {zone_str}")
    68→
    69→    report = capability_report(lang)
    70→    if report is not None:
    71→        present, missing = report
    72→        if present:
    73→            _stderr(f"  Capabilities: {', '.join(present)}")
    74→        if missing:
    75→            _stderr(f"  Not available: {', '.join(missing)}")
    76→
    77→
    78→def _select_phases(lang: LangRun, *, include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    79→    active_profile = profile if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"} else "full"
    80→    phases = lang.phases
    81→    if not include_slow or active_profile == "ci":
    82→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not phase.slow]
    83→    if active_profile in {"objective", "ci"}:
    84→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not is_subjective_phase(phase)]
    85→    return phases
    86→
    87→
    88→def _run_phases(path: Path, lang: LangRun, phases: list[DetectorPhase]) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    89→    issues: list[Issue] = []
    90→    all_potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    91→
    92→    total = len(phases)
    93→    for idx, phase in enumerate(phases, start=1):
    94→        _stderr(f"  [{idx}/{total}] {phase.label}...")
    95→        phase_issues, phase_potentials = phase.run(path, lang)
    96→        all_potentials.update(phase_potentials)
    97→        issues.extend(phase_issues)
    98→
    99→    return issues, all_potentials
   100→
```

```
   102→def _stamp_issue_context(issues: list[Issue], lang: LangRun) -> None:
   103→    if not issues:
   104→        return
   105→
   106→    zone_policies = None
   107→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
   108→        zone_policies = ZONE_POLICIES
   109→
   110→    for issue in issues:
   111→        issue["lang"] = lang.name
   112→        if lang.zone_map is None:
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        zone = lang.zone_map.get(issue.get("file", ""))
   116→        issue["zone"] = zone.value
   117→        policy = zone_policies.get(zone) if zone_policies else None
   118→        if policy and issue.get("detector") in policy.downgrade_detectors:
   119→            issue["confidence"] = "low"
   120→
   121→
   122→def _generate_issues_from_lang(
   123→    path: Path,
   124→    lang: LangRun,
   125→    *,
   126→    include_slow: bool = True,
   127→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   128→    profile: str = "full",
   129→) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   130→    """Run detector phases from a LangRun."""
   131→    _build_zone_map(path, lang, zone_overrides)
   132→    phases = _select_phases(lang, include_slow=include_slow, profile=profile)
   133→    issues, all_potentials = _run_phases(path, lang, phases)
   134→    _stamp_issue_context(issues, lang)
   135→    _stderr(f"\n  Total: {len(issues)} issues")
   136→    return issues, all_potentials
   137→
   138→
   139→def generate_issues(
   140→    path: Path,
   141→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None = None,
   142→    *,
   143→    options: PlanScanOptions | None = None,
   144→) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   145→    """Run all detectors and convert results to normalized issues."""
   146→    resolved_options = options or PlanScanOptions()
   147→
   148→    resolved_lang = _resolve_lang(lang, get_project_root())
   149→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(resolved_lang)
   150→    return _generate_issues_from_lang(
   151→        path,
   152→        runtime_lang,
   153→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   154→        zone_overrides=resolved_options.zone_overrides,
   155→        profile=resolved_options.profile,
   156→    )
   157→
```

```
1→"""Path resolution and exclusion matching helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→import os
     7→import tempfile
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    11→
    12→
    13→def matches_exclusion(rel_path: str, exclusion: str) -> bool:
    14→    """Check if a relative path matches an exclusion pattern."""
    15→    parts = Path(rel_path).parts
    16→    if exclusion in parts:
    17→        return True
    18→    if "*" in exclusion:
    19→        if any(fnmatch.fnmatch(part, exclusion) for part in parts):
    20→            return True
    21→        # Full-path glob match for patterns with directory separators
    22→        # (e.g. "Wan2GP/**" should match "Wan2GP/models/rf.py").
    23→        if "/" in exclusion or os.sep in exclusion:
    24→            normalized_path = rel_path.lstrip("./")
    25→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(normalized_path, exclusion):
    26→                return True
    27→    if "/" in exclusion or os.sep in exclusion:
    28→        normalized = exclusion.rstrip("/").rstrip(os.sep)
    29→        return rel_path == normalized or rel_path.startswith(normalized + "/") or rel_path.startswith(
    30→            normalized + os.sep
    31→        )
    32→    return False
    33→
    34→
    35→def normalize_path_separators(path: str) -> str:
    36→    return path.replace("\\", "/")
    37→
    38→
    39→def safe_relpath(path: str | Path, start: str | Path) -> str:
    40→    try:
    41→        return os.path.relpath(str(path), str(start))
    42→    except ValueError:
    43→        return str(Path(path).resolve())
    44→
    45→
    46→def rel(path: str | Path, *, project_root: str | Path | None = None) -> str:
    47→    """Return a normalized project-relative path when possible."""
    48→    root = get_project_root(project_root=project_root)
    49→    resolved = Path(path).resolve()
    50→    try:
    51→        return normalize_path_separators(str(resolved.relative_to(root)))
    52→    except ValueError:
    53→        return normalize_path_separators(safe_relpath(resolved, root))
    54→
    55→
    56→def resolve_path(filepath: str, *, project_root: str | Path | None = None) -> str:
    57→    """Resolve a filepath to absolute, handling both relative and absolute."""
    58→    p = Path(filepath)
    59→    if p.is_absolute():
    60→        return str(p.resolve())
    61→    return str((get_project_root(project_root=project_root) / filepath).resolve())
    62→
    63→
    64→def resolve_scan_file(
    65→    filepath: str | Path,
    66→    *,
    67→    scan_root: str | Path | None = None,
    68→    project_root: str | Path | None = None,
    69→) -> Path:
    70→    """Resolve a scan file path with explicit scan-root-first semantics.
    71→
    72→    Relative file paths are resolved against ``scan_root`` first (when provided)
    73→    and then against the process project root as a fallback.
    74→    """
    75→    p = Path(filepath)
    76→    if p.is_absolute():
    77→        return p.resolve()
    78→
    79→    root = get_project_root(project_root=project_root)
    80→    if scan_root is not None:
    81→        scan_root_path = Path(scan_root)
    82→        scan_root_abs = (
    83→            scan_root_path.resolve()
    84→            if scan_root_path.is_absolute()
    85→            else (root / scan_root_path).resolve()
    86→        )
    87→        scan_candidate = (scan_root_abs / p).resolve()
    88→        if scan_candidate.exists():
    89→            return scan_candidate
    90→
    91→    return (root / p).resolve()
    92→
    93→
    94→def safe_write_text(filepath: str | Path, content: str) -> None:
    95→    """Atomically write text to a file using temp+rename."""
    96→    p = Path(filepath)
    97→    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    98→    fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=p.parent, suffix=".tmp")
    99→    try:
   100→        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   101→            f.write(content)
   102→        os.replace(tmp, str(p))
   103→    except OSError:
   104→        if os.path.exists(tmp):
   105→            os.unlink(tmp)
   106→        raise
   107→
   108→
   109→def count_lines(path: Path) -> int:
   110→    """Count lines in a file without loading full contents into memory."""
   111→    count = 0
   112→    try:
   113→        with path.open("rb") as handle:
   114→            for _ in handle:
   115→                count += 1
   116→    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
   117→        return 0
   118→    return count
   119→
   120→
   121→__all__ = [
   122→    "count_lines",
   123→    "matches_exclusion",
   124→    "normalize_path_separators",
   125→    "rel",
   126→    "resolve_path",
   127→    "resolve_scan_file",
   128→    "safe_relpath",
   129→    "safe_write_text",
   130→]
   131→
```

```
   104→def _batch_live_log_interval_seconds(heartbeat_seconds: float) -> float:
   105→    """Clamp the live log polling interval derived from the heartbeat."""
   106→    if heartbeat_seconds <= 0:
   107→        return 5.0
   108→    return max(1.0, min(heartbeat_seconds, 10.0))

   111→def _build_batch_run_deps(*, policy, project_root: Path) -> review_batches_mod.BatchRunDeps:
   112→    """Build the dependency bundle used by prepare/execute/import phases."""
   113→    from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_policy_result, render_policy_block
   114→
   115→    policy_result = load_policy_result()
   116→    policy_block = render_policy_block(policy_result.policy)
   117→    if not policy_result.ok:
   118→        print(
   119→            colorize(
   120→                f"  Warning: ignoring malformed project policy ({policy_result.message or 'unknown error'}).",
   121→                "yellow",
   122→            )
   123→        )
   124→    codex_batch_deps = CodexBatchRunnerDeps(
   125→        timeout_seconds=policy.batch_timeout_seconds,
   126→        subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   127→        timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   128→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   129→        use_popen_runner=(getattr(subprocess.run, "__module__", "") == "subprocess"),
   130→        subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,
   131→        live_log_interval_seconds=_batch_live_log_interval_seconds(
   132→            policy.heartbeat_seconds
   133→        ),
   134→        stall_after_output_seconds=policy.stall_kill_seconds,
   135→        max_retries=policy.batch_max_retries,
   136→        retry_backoff_seconds=policy.batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   137→    )
   138→    followup_scan_deps = FollowupScanDeps(
   139→        project_root=project_root,
   140→        timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   141→        python_executable=sys.executable,
   142→        subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   143→        timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   144→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   145→    )
   146→    return review_batches_mod.BatchRunDeps(
   147→        run_stamp_fn=run_stamp,
   148→        load_or_prepare_packet_fn=_load_or_prepare_packet,
   149→        selected_batch_indexes_fn=lambda args, batch_count: selected_batch_indexes(
   150→            raw_selection=getattr(args, "only_batches", None),
   151→            batch_count=batch_count,

   201→
   202→def _build_prepared_packet_contract(args, *, config: dict | None) -> dict[str, object]:
   203→    """Build normalized invocation contract for prepared packet reuse."""
   204→    return prepared_packet_contract(resolve_review_packet_context(args), config=config)
   205→
   206→
   207→def _prepared_packet_contract_mismatch_reason(
   208→    packet: dict[str, object],
   209→    expected_contract: dict[str, object],
   210→) -> str | None:
   211→    """Return mismatch reason for prepared packet reuse, else ``None``."""
   212→    raw_contract = packet.get(_PREPARED_PACKET_CONTRACT_KEY)
   213→    if not isinstance(raw_contract, dict):
   214→        return "missing prepared packet contract metadata"
   215→
   216→    for key in (
   217→        "path",
   218→        "dimensions",
   219→        "retrospective",
   220→        "retrospective_max_issues",
   221→        "retrospective_max_batch_items",
   222→        "config_hash",
   223→    ):
   224→        if raw_contract.get(key) != expected_contract.get(key):
   225→            return f"contract field '{key}' differs"
   226→    return None
   227→
   228→
   229→def _try_load_prepared_packet(
   230→    *,
   231→    expected_contract: dict[str, object],
   232→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
   233→    """Load prepared packet from query.json when shape and contract match."""
   234→    try:
   235→        qf = query_file_path()
   236→        if not qf.exists():
   237→            return None, None
   238→        data = json.loads(qf.read_text())
   239→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, RuntimeError):
   240→        return None, "query.json is missing or invalid JSON"
   241→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   242→        return None, "prepared packet payload is not an object"
   243→    if "investigation_batches" not in data:
   244→        return None, "prepared packet is missing investigation batches"
   245→    batches = data["investigation_batches"]
   246→    if not isinstance(batches, list) or not batches:
   247→        return None, "prepared packet has no investigation batches"
   248→
   249→    mismatch_reason = _prepared_packet_contract_mismatch_reason(
   250→        data,
   251→        expected_contract,
   252→    )
   253→    if mismatch_reason is not None:
   254→        return None, mismatch_reason
   255→    return data, None
   256→
   257→
   258→def _merge_batch_results(batch_results: list[object]) -> dict[str, object]:
   259→    """Deterministically merge assessments/issues across batch outputs."""
   260→    normalized_results: list[BatchResultPayload] = []
   261→    for result in batch_results:
   262→        if hasattr(result, "to_dict") and callable(result.to_dict):
   263→            payload = result.to_dict()
   264→            if isinstance(payload, dict):
   265→                normalized_results.append(cast(BatchResultPayload, payload))
   266→                continue
   267→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   268→            normalized_results.append(cast(BatchResultPayload, result))
   269→    return merge_batch_results(
   270→        normalized_results,
   271→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   272→        abstraction_component_names=ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES,
   273→    )
   274→
   275→
   276→def _load_or_prepare_packet(
   277→    args,
   278→    *,
   279→    state: dict,
   280→    lang,
   281→    config: dict,
   282→    stamp: str,
   283→) -> tuple[dict, Path, Path]:
   284→    """Load packet override or prepare a fresh packet snapshot."""
   285→    packet_override = getattr(args, "packet", None)
   286→    if packet_override:
   287→        packet_path = Path(packet_override)
   288→        if not packet_path.exists():
   289→            raise PacketValidationError(f"packet not found: {packet_override}", exit_code=1)
   290→        try:
   291→            packet = json.loads(packet_path.read_text())
   292→        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   293→            raise PacketValidationError(f"reading packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   294→        blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   295→        blind_packet = build_blind_packet(packet)
   296→        safe_write_text(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   297→        print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   298→        print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_path}", "dim"))
   299→        return packet, packet_path, blind_path
   300→
   301→    # When no explicit --packet and no explicit --dimensions were given,
   302→    # check whether a prior ``review --prepare`` left a valid query.json
   303→    # packet we can reuse instead of rebuilding from scratch.
   304→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
   305→
   306→    # Validate explicit dimensions against the language's scored dimensions.
   307→    if dims:
   308→        lang_obj = lang
   309→        lang_name = getattr(lang_obj, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang_obj, "lang", ""))
   310→        if lang_name:
   311→            valid_dims = set(scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name))
   312→            if valid_dims:
   313→                invalid = sorted(dims - valid_dims)
   314→                if invalid:
   315→                    valid_list = ", ".join(sorted(valid_dims))
   316→                    raise CommandError(
   317→                        f"Invalid dimensions for language '{lang_name}': {', '.join(invalid)}. "
   318→                        f"Valid dimensions: {valid_list}",
   319→                        exit_code=1,
   320→                    )
   321→
   322→    expected_contract = _build_prepared_packet_contract(args, config=config)
   323→    if not dims:
   324→        prepared, mismatch_reason = _try_load_prepared_packet(
   325→            expected_contract=expected_contract,
   326→        )
   327→        if prepared is not None:
   328→            print(colorize("  Reusing prepared packet from query.json", "dim"))
   329→            blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   330→            blind_packet = build_blind_packet(prepared)
   331→            safe_write_text(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   332→            packet_path, blind_saved = write_packet_snapshot(
   333→                prepared,
   334→                stamp=stamp,
   335→                review_packet_dir=_review_packet_dir(),
   336→                blind_path=blind_path,
   337→                safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   338→            )
   339→            print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   340→            print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_saved}", "dim"))
   341→            return prepared, packet_path, blind_saved
   342→        if mismatch_reason:
   343→            print(
   344→                colorize(
   345→                    f"  Prepared packet reuse rejected: {mismatch_reason}; rebuilding.",
   346→                    "dim",
   347→                )
   348→            )
   349→
   350→    context = resolve_review_packet_context(args)
   351→    blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   352→    packet, _lang_name = build_holistic_packet(
   353→        state=state,
   354→        lang=lang,
   355→        config=config,
   356→        context=context,
   357→        setup_lang_fn=_setup_lang,
   358→        prepare_holistic_review_fn=prepare_holistic_review,
   359→    )
   360→    packet["config"] = redacted_review_config(config)
   361→    packet[_PREPARED_PACKET_CONTRACT_KEY] = expected_contract
   362→    packet["next_command"] = build_run_batches_next_command(context)
   363→    write_query_best_effort(
   364→        packet,
   365→        context="review packet query update",
   366→    )
   367→    packet_path, blind_saved = write_packet_snapshot(
   368→        packet,
   369→        stamp=stamp,
   370→        review_packet_dir=_review_packet_dir(),
   371→        blind_path=blind_path,
   372→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   373→    )
   374→    print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   375→    print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_saved}", "dim"))
   376→    return packet, packet_path, blind_saved
   377→
   378→
   379→def do_run_batches(args, state, lang, state_file, config: dict | None = None) -> None:
   380→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   381→    from ..runtime.policy import resolve_batch_run_policy  # noqa: PLC0415
   382→
   383→    project_root = _runtime_project_root()
   384→    subagent_runs_dir = _subagent_runs_dir()
   385→    policy = resolve_batch_run_policy(args)
   386→    batch_deps = _build_batch_run_deps(
   387→        policy=policy,
   388→        project_root=project_root,
   389→    )
   390→    prepared = review_batch_phases_mod.prepare_batch_run(
   391→        args=args,
   392→        state=state,
   393→        lang=lang,
   394→        config=config or {},
   395→        deps=batch_deps,
   396→        project_root=project_root,
   397→        subagent_runs_dir=subagent_runs_dir,
   398→    )
   399→    if prepared is None:
   400→        return
   401→
   402→    executed = review_batch_phases_mod.execute_batch_run(
   403→        prepared=prepared,
   404→        deps=batch_deps,
   405→    )
   406→    review_batch_phases_mod.merge_and_import_batch_run(
   407→        prepared=prepared,
   408→        executed=executed,
   409→        state_file=state_file,
   410→        deps=batch_deps,
   411→    )
   412→
   413→def _validate_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, Path, str]:
   414→    """Validate run directory, load summary, and return (summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path).
   415→
   416→    Raises CommandError on any validation failure.
   417→    """
   418→    if not run_dir.is_dir():
   419→        raise CommandError(f"run directory not found: {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   420→
   421→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   422→    if not summary_path.exists():
   423→        raise CommandError(f"no run_summary.json in {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   424→    try:
   425→        summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text())
   426→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   427→        raise CommandError(f"Error reading run summary: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   428→
   429→    selected = summary.get("selected_batches", [])
   430→    blind_packet_path = Path(str(summary.get("blind_packet", "")))
   431→    immutable_packet_path = str(summary.get("immutable_packet", ""))
   432→
   433→    if not selected:
   434→        raise CommandError("no selected batches in run summary.", exit_code=1)
   435→    if not blind_packet_path.exists():
   436→        raise PacketValidationError(f"blind packet not found: {blind_packet_path}", exit_code=1)
   437→
   438→    try:
   439→        packet = json.loads(Path(immutable_packet_path).read_text())
   440→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   441→        raise PacketValidationError(f"Error reading immutable packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   442→
   443→    summary["_packet"] = packet
   444→    return summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path
   445→
   446→
   447→def do_import_run(
   448→    run_dir_path: str,
   449→    state: dict,
   450→    lang,
   451→    state_file: str,
   452→    *,
   453→    config: dict | None = None,
   454→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   455→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   456→    scan_path: str = ".",
   457→    dry_run: bool = False,
   458→) -> None:
   459→    """Re-import results from a completed run directory.
   460→
   461→    Replays the merge+provenance+import step that normally runs at the end of
   462→    ``--run-batches``.  Useful when the original pipeline was interrupted (e.g.
   463→    broken pipe from background execution) but all batch results completed.
   464→    """
   465→    run_dir = Path(run_dir_path)
   466→    summary, blind_packet_path, _immutable_path = _validate_run_dir(run_dir)
   467→
   468→    runner = str(summary.get("runner", "codex"))
   469→    stamp = str(summary.get("run_stamp", ""))
   470→    selected = summary.get("selected_batches", [])
   471→    packet = summary.pop("_packet", {})
   472→    allowed_dims = {str(d) for d in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(d, str)}
   473→
   474→    # -- locate and parse raw batch results --
   475→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   476→    selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in selected]  # convert 1-based to 0-based
   477→    output_files = {
   478→        idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   479→        for idx in selected_indexes
   480→    }
   481→
   482→    missing = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes if not output_files[idx].exists()]
   483→    if missing:
   484→        raise CommandError(f"missing result files for batches: {missing}", exit_code=1)
   485→
   486→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results(
   487→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   488→        failures=[],
   489→        output_files=output_files,
   490→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   491→        extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   492→        normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: normalize_batch_result(
   493→            payload,
   494→            dims,
   495→            max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
   496→            abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   497→        ),
   498→    )
   499→
   500→    if not batch_results:
   501→        raise CommandError("no valid batch results could be parsed.", exit_code=1)
   502→
   503→    print(colorize(f"  Parsed {len(batch_results)} batch results from {run_dir}", "bold"))
   504→    if failures:
   505→        print(colorize(f"  Warning: {len(failures)} batches failed to parse: {[f + 1 for f in failures]}", "yellow"))
   506→
   507→    successful_indexes = [idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in set(failures)]
   508→
   509→    # Reuse the canonical merge+metadata boundary from normal batch execution.
   510→    raw_batches = packet.get("investigation_batches", [])
   511→    raw_dim_prompts = packet.get("dimension_prompts")
   512→    batches = explode_to_single_dimension(
   513→        raw_batches if isinstance(raw_batches, list) else [],
   514→        dimension_prompts=raw_dim_prompts if isinstance(raw_dim_prompts, dict) else None,
   515→    )
   516→    packet_dimensions = normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   517→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   518→    scored_dimensions = scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name) if lang_name else []
   519→    merged_path, missing_after_import = _merge_and_write_results(
   520→        merge_batch_results_fn=_merge_batch_results,
   521→        build_import_provenance_fn=build_batch_import_provenance,
   522→        batch_results=batch_results,
   523→        batches=batches,
   524→        successful_indexes=successful_indexes,
   525→        packet=packet,
   526→        packet_dimensions=packet_dimensions,
   527→        scored_dimensions=scored_dimensions,
   528→        scan_path=scan_path,
   529→        runner=runner,
   530→        prompt_packet_path=blind_packet_path,
   531→        stamp=stamp,
   532→        run_dir=run_dir,
   533→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   534→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   535→    )
   536→    _enforce_import_coverage(
   537→        missing_after_import=missing_after_import,
   538→        packet_dimensions=packet_dimensions,
   539→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   540→        scan_path=scan_path,
   541→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   542→    )
   543→
   544→    # -- import with trusted source --
   545→    _do_import(
   546→        str(merged_path),
   547→        state,
   548→        lang,
   549→        state_file,
   550→        import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   551→            config=config,
   552→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   553→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   554→            trusted_assessment_label=f"trusted import-run replay from {run_dir.name}",
   555→        ),
   556→        dry_run=dry_run,
   557→    )
   558→
   559→    # -- optional follow-up scan --
   560→    if scan_after_import and not dry_run:
   561→        lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   562→        if lang_name:
   563→            run_followup_scan(
   564→                lang_name=lang_name,
   565→                scan_path=scan_path,
   566→                deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   567→                    project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   568→                    timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   569→                    python_executable=sys.executable,
   570→                    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   571→                    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   572→                    colorize_fn=colorize,
   573→                ),
   574→            )
   575→
```

```
    64→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    65→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    66→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    67→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    68→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    69→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    70→            continue
    71→        try:
    72→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    73→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:

   102→        raise CommandError(
   103→            "Error: choose one review mode per command "
   104→            "(--merge | --run-batches | --import-run | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
   105→            exit_code=1,
   106→        )
   107→
   108→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.import_file:
   109→        raise CommandError(
   110→            "Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
   111→            exit_code=2,
   112→        )
   113→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.session_id:
   114→        raise CommandError(
   115→            "Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
   116→            exit_code=2,
   117→        )
   118→
   119→
   120→def _is_default_prepare_mode(opts: ReviewOptions, mode_flags: list[bool]) -> bool:
   121→    """True when the command will run the default prepare flow (no explicit mode)."""
   122→    return not any(mode_flags) and not opts.import_file
   123→
   124→
   125→def _run_review_mode(
   126→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   127→    *,
   128→    opts: ReviewOptions,
   129→    runtime,
   130→    state,
   131→    lang,
   132→    state_file,
   133→) -> None:
   134→    if opts.merge:
   135→        do_merge(args)
   136→        return
   137→    if opts.run_batches:
   138→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   139→        do_run_batches(
   140→            args,
   141→            state,
   142→            lang,
   143→            state_file,
   144→            config=runtime.config,
   145→        )
   146→        return
   147→    if opts.import_run_dir:
   148→        do_import_run(
   149→            opts.import_run_dir,
   150→            state,
   151→            lang,
   152→            state_file,
   153→            config=runtime.config,
   154→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   155→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   156→            scan_path=opts.path,
   157→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   158→        )
   159→        return
   160→    if opts.external_start:
   161→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   162→        do_external_start(
   163→            args,
   164→            state,
   165→            lang,
   166→            config=runtime.config,
   167→        )
   168→        return
   169→    if opts.external_submit:
   170→        do_external_submit(
   171→            import_file=str(opts.import_file),
   172→            session_id=str(opts.session_id),
   173→            state=state,
   174→            lang=lang,
   175→            state_file=state_file,
   176→            config=runtime.config,
   177→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   178→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   179→            scan_path=opts.path,
   180→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   181→        )
   182→        return
   183→    if opts.validate_import_file:
   184→        do_validate_import(
   185→            opts.validate_import_file,
   186→            lang,
   187→            import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   188→                allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   189→                manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   190→                attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   191→                manual_attest=opts.attest,
   192→            ),
   193→        )
   194→        return
   195→
   196→    if opts.import_file:
   197→        do_import(
   198→            opts.import_file,
   199→            state,
   200→            lang,
   201→            state_file,
   202→            import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   203→                config=runtime.config,
   204→                allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   205→                manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   206→                attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   207→                manual_attest=opts.attest,
   208→            ),
   209→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   210→        )
   211→        return
   212→    review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   213→    do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   214→
   215→
   216→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   217→    """Prepare or import subjective code review issues."""
   218→    _enable_live_review_output()
   219→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   220→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   221→    state = runtime.state
   222→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   223→    _require_lang(lang)
   224→
   225→    opts = ReviewOptions.from_args(args)
   226→    mode_flags = _mode_flags(opts)
   227→    _validate_mode_selection(
   228→        opts,
   229→        mode_flags=mode_flags,
   230→    )
   231→
   232→    # Default prepare and --run-batches need real scan-backed context rather
   233→    # than reconstructed plan inventory.
   234→    if _is_default_prepare_mode(opts, mode_flags) or opts.run_batches:
   235→        if not require_scan_metrics(state):
   236→            return
   237→
   238→    _run_review_mode(
   239→        args,
   240→        opts=opts,
   241→        runtime=runtime,
   242→        state=state,
   243→        lang=lang,
   244→        state_file=state_file,
   245→    )
   246→
```

```
    32→class _DetectorNamesCacheCompat:
    33→    """Compat shim for tests that poke the legacy detector-name cache."""
    34→
    35→    def __init__(self) -> None:
    36→        self._store: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    37→
    38→    def __contains__(self, key: object) -> bool:
    39→        return key in self._store
    40→
    41→    def __getitem__(self, key: str) -> list[str]:
    42→        return self._store[key]
    43→
    44→    def __setitem__(self, key: str, value: list[str]) -> None:
    45→        self._store[key] = value
    46→
    47→    def pop(self, key: str, default: list[str] | None = None) -> list[str] | None:
    48→        return self._store.pop(key, default)
    49→
    50→
    51→_DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE = _DetectorNamesCacheCompat()
    52→
    53→
    54→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
    55→def _get_detector_names_cached() -> tuple[str, ...]:
    56→    """Compute detector names once until cache invalidation."""
    57→    return tuple(detector_names())
    58→
    59→
    60→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    61→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    62→    return list(_get_detector_names_cached())
    63→
    64→
    65→def _invalidate_detector_names_cache() -> None:
    66→    """Invalidate detector-name cache when runtime registrations change."""
    67→    _get_detector_names_cached.cache_clear()
    68→    _DETECTOR_NAMES_CACHE.pop("names", None)
    69→

    71→on_detector_registered(_invalidate_detector_names_cache)

    74→def create_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    75→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""

    77→
    78→
    79→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(
    80→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    81→    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    82→) -> None:
    83→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    84→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    85→    persisted_raw = config.get("exclude", [])
    86→    persisted = (
    87→        [entry for entry in persisted_raw if isinstance(entry, str)]
    88→        if isinstance(persisted_raw, list)
    89→        else []
    90→    )
    91→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    92→    if not combined:
    93→        return
    94→    set_exclusions(combined)
    95→    if cli_exclusions:
    96→        print(
    97→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    98→            file=sys.stderr,
    99→        )
   100→        return
   101→    print(
   102→        colorize(
   103→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
   104→        ),
   105→        file=sys.stderr,
   106→    )
   107→
   108→
   109→def _project_root_from_state_path(state_path_value: str | Path | None) -> Path | None:
   110→    """Infer project root from an explicit state path under ``.desloppify``."""
   111→    if state_path_value in (None, ""):
   112→        return None
   113→    try:
   114→        state_file = Path(state_path_value).resolve()
   115→    except OSError:
   116→        return None
   117→    if state_file.parent.name != ".desloppify":
   118→        return None
   119→    if state_file.name == "state.json" or (
   120→        state_file.name.startswith("state-") and state_file.suffix == ".json"
   121→    ):
   122→        return state_file.parent.parent
   123→    return None
   124→
   125→
   126→def _resolve_default_path(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   127→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
   128→
   129→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
   130→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
   131→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
   132→    """
   133→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
   134→        return
   135→    runtime_root = get_project_root()
   136→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
   137→        try:
   138→            state_file = state_path(args)
   139→            if state_file:
   140→                saved = load_state(state_file)
   141→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
   142→                if saved_path:
   143→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
   144→                    return
   145→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
   146→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "resolve default review path from saved state", exc)
   147→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   148→    args.path = str(
   149→        get_default_scan_path(
   150→            project_root=runtime_root,
   151→            default_src=lang.default_src if lang else None,
   152→        )
   153→    )
   154→
   155→
   156→def _load_shared_runtime(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   157→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
   158→    config = load_config()
   159→
   160→    state_file = state_path(args)
   161→    state = load_state(state_file)
   162→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   163→
   164→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   165→
   166→
   167→def _looks_like_desloppify_checkout(root: Path) -> bool:
   168→    """Return True when *root* appears to be the desloppify source checkout."""
   169→    package_dir = root / "desloppify"
   170→    pyproject = root / "pyproject.toml"
   171→    if not package_dir.is_dir() or not (package_dir / "__init__.py").is_file():
   172→        return False
   173→    if not pyproject.is_file():
   174→        return False
   175→    try:
   176→        text = pyproject.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   177→    except OSError:
   178→        return False
   179→    return 'name = "desloppify"' in text or "name='desloppify'" in text
   180→
   181→
   182→def _running_installed_package_from_checkout(
   183→    *,
   184→    cwd_root: Path | None = None,
   185→    module_file: str | Path | None = None,
   186→) -> bool:
   187→    """Detect running an installed package while standing in a repo checkout."""
   188→    root = (cwd_root or get_project_root()).resolve()
   189→    if not _looks_like_desloppify_checkout(root):
   190→        return False
   191→    current_module = Path(module_file or __file__).resolve()
   192→    try:
   193→        current_module.relative_to(root)
   194→        return False
   195→    except ValueError:
   196→        return True
   197→
   198→
   199→def _warn_if_running_installed_package_from_checkout() -> None:
   200→    """Emit a warning when CLI resolution bypasses the current checkout."""
   201→    if not _running_installed_package_from_checkout():
   202→        return
   203→    root = get_project_root().resolve()
   204→    print(
   205→        colorize(
   206→            "  WARNING: running installed desloppify package while current directory "
   207→            "looks like the desloppify checkout.",
   208→            "yellow",
   209→        ),
   210→        file=sys.stderr,
   211→    )
   212→    print(
   213→        colorize(
   214→            f"  Current checkout: {root}",
   215→            "dim",
   216→        ),
   217→        file=sys.stderr,
   218→    )
   219→    print(
   220→        colorize(
   221→            "  Use `python -m desloppify ...` or `./.venv/bin/desloppify ...` here "
   222→            "to run the local checkout instead of the installed package.",
   223→            "dim",
   224→        ),
   225→        file=sys.stderr,
   226→    )
   227→
   228→
   229→def _resolve_handler(command: str) -> CommandHandler:
   230→    """Resolve a CLI command name to its typed handler callable."""
   231→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   232→
   233→
   234→def _handle_help_command(
   235→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   236→    parser: argparse.ArgumentParser,
   237→) -> None:
   238→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   239→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   240→    try:
   241→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   242→    except SystemExit:
   243→        return
   244→
   245→
   246→def main() -> None:
   247→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   248→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   249→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   250→            try:
   251→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   252→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   253→                logger.debug(
   254→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   255→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   256→                )
   257→
   258→    parser = create_parser()
   259→    args = parser.parse_args()
   260→    if not args.command:
   261→        parser.print_help()
   262→        return
   263→    if args.command == "help":
   264→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   265→        return
   266→
   267→    try:
   268→        with runtime_scope() as runtime:
   269→            inferred = _project_root_from_state_path(getattr(args, "state", None))
   270→            if inferred is not None:
   271→                runtime.project_root = inferred
   272→            _warn_if_running_installed_package_from_checkout()
   273→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   274→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   275→
   276→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   277→            handler(args)
   278→    except CommandError as exc:
   279→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   280→        sys.exit(exc.exit_code)
   281→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   282→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   283→        sys.exit(1)
   284→
   285→
   286→if __name__ == "__main__":
   287→    main()
   288→
```

```
    28→class TestMatchPattern:
    29→    """Tests for the _match_pattern helper."""
    30→
    31→    def test_directory_pattern_matches(self):
    32→        """'/tests/' matches a file inside a tests directory."""
    33→        assert _match_pattern("src/tests/foo.py", "/tests/") is True
    34→
    35→    def test_directory_pattern_at_root(self):
    36→        """'/tests/' matches a file at the root-level tests dir."""
    37→        assert _match_pattern("tests/foo.py", "/tests/") is True
    38→
    39→    def test_directory_pattern_no_false_positive(self):
    40→        """'/tests/' does NOT match 'attestation.py' (substring only)."""
    41→        assert _match_pattern("attestation.py", "/tests/") is False
    42→
    43→    def test_directory_pattern_nested(self):
    44→        """'/vendor/' matches a deeply nested vendor directory."""
    45→        assert _match_pattern("lib/vendor/pkg/file.js", "/vendor/") is True
    46→
    47→    def test_directory_pattern_must_be_component(self):
    48→        """'/test/' does not match 'testimony/foo.py' — needs directory boundary."""
    49→        assert _match_pattern("testimony/foo.py", "/test/") is False
    50→
    51→    def test_suffix_extension_pattern(self):
    52→        """'.test.' matches 'foo.test.ts' via contains on basename."""
    53→        assert _match_pattern("src/foo.test.ts", ".test.") is True
    54→
    55→    def test_suffix_extension_pattern_tsx(self):
    56→        """'.test.' matches 'Component.test.tsx'."""
    57→        assert _match_pattern("components/Component.test.tsx", ".test.") is True
    58→
    59→    def test_suffix_extension_pattern_no_match(self):
    60→        """'.test.' does NOT match 'test_utils.py' (no '.test.' substring)."""
    61→        assert _match_pattern("test_utils.py", ".test.") is False
    62→
    63→    def test_dot_extension_pattern(self):
    64→        """'.d.ts' matches 'types.d.ts' via contains on basename."""
    65→        assert _match_pattern("src/types.d.ts", ".d.ts") is True
    66→
    67→    def test_dot_extension_no_match(self):
    68→        """'.d.ts' does NOT match 'data.ts'."""
    69→        assert _match_pattern("src/data.ts", ".d.ts") is False
    70→
    71→    def test_prefix_pattern_matches(self):
    72→        """'test_' matches 'test_foo.py' — basename starts with prefix."""
    73→        assert _match_pattern("test_foo.py", "test_") is True
    74→
    75→    def test_prefix_pattern_with_directory(self):
    76→        """'test_' matches 'src/test_bar.py'."""
    77→        assert _match_pattern("src/test_bar.py", "test_") is True
    78→
    79→    def test_prefix_pattern_no_match(self):
    80→        """'test_' does NOT match 'contest_results.py' — no prefix match."""
    81→        assert _match_pattern("contest_results.py", "test_") is False
    82→
    83→    def test_suffix_underscore_pattern(self):
    84→        """'_test.py' matches 'foo_test.py' — basename ends with suffix."""
    85→        assert _match_pattern("foo_test.py", "_test.py") is True
    86→
    87→    def test_suffix_underscore_pattern_with_dir(self):
    88→        """'_test.py' matches 'src/bar_test.py'."""
    89→        assert _match_pattern("src/bar_test.py", "_test.py") is True
    90→
    91→    def test_suffix_underscore_no_match(self):
    92→        """'_test.py' does NOT match 'test_foo.py'."""
    93→        assert _match_pattern("test_foo.py", "_test.py") is False
    94→
    95→    def test_suffix_pb2(self):
    96→        """'_pb2.py' matches 'message_pb2.py'."""
    97→        assert _match_pattern("protos/message_pb2.py", "_pb2.py") is True
    98→
    99→    def test_exact_basename_match(self):
   100→        """'config.py' matches 'src/config.py' — exact basename."""
   101→        assert _match_pattern("src/config.py", "config.py") is True
   102→
   103→    def test_exact_basename_no_match(self):
   104→        """'config.py' does NOT match 'src/myconfig.py'."""
   105→        assert _match_pattern("src/myconfig.py", "config.py") is False
   106→
   107→    def test_exact_basename_conftest(self):
   108→        """'conftest.py' matches 'tests/conftest.py'."""
   109→        assert _match_pattern("tests/conftest.py", "conftest.py") is True
   110→
   111→    def test_exact_basename_setup(self):
   112→        """'setup.py' matches 'setup.py' at root."""
   113→        assert _match_pattern("setup.py", "setup.py") is True
   114→
   115→    def test_fallback_substring_match(self):
   116→        """Fallback substring matches on full path (e.g. 'tsconfig')."""
   117→        assert _match_pattern("project/tsconfig.json", "tsconfig") is True
   118→
   119→    def test_fallback_substring_no_match(self):
   120→        """Fallback substring does NOT match unrelated paths."""
   121→        assert _match_pattern("src/app.ts", "tsconfig") is False
   122→
   123→    def test_fallback_vite_config(self):
   124→        """'vite.config' matches 'vite.config.ts' via fallback substring."""
   125→        assert _match_pattern("vite.config.ts", "vite.config") is True
   126→

   131→class TestClassifyFile:
   132→    """Tests for the classify_file function."""
   133→
   134→    def test_vendor_directory(self):
   135→        """File in vendor/ is classified as VENDOR."""
   136→        assert classify_file("lib/vendor/pkg.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.VENDOR
   137→
   138→    def test_third_party_directory(self):
   139→        """File in third_party/ is classified as VENDOR."""
   140→        assert classify_file("third_party/lib.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.VENDOR
   141→
   142→    def test_vendored_directory(self):
   143→        """File in vendored/ is classified as VENDOR."""
   144→        assert classify_file("vendored/dep.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.VENDOR
   145→
   146→    def test_generated_directory(self):
   147→        """File in generated/ is classified as GENERATED."""
   148→        assert (
   149→            classify_file("src/generated/types.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.GENERATED
   150→        )
   151→
   152→    def test_dunder_generated_directory(self):
   153→        """File in __generated__/ is classified as GENERATED."""
   154→        assert (
   155→            classify_file("__generated__/schema.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES)
   156→            == Zone.GENERATED
   157→        )
   158→
   159→    def test_tests_directory(self):
   160→        """File in tests/ is classified as TEST."""
   161→        assert classify_file("tests/test_foo.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.TEST
   162→
   163→    def test_test_directory(self):
   164→        """File in test/ is classified as TEST."""
   165→        assert classify_file("test/test_bar.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.TEST
   166→
   167→    def test_fixtures_directory(self):
   168→        """File in fixtures/ is classified as TEST."""
   169→        assert classify_file("fixtures/data.json", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.TEST
   170→
   171→    def test_scripts_directory(self):
   172→        """File in scripts/ is classified as SCRIPT."""
   173→        assert classify_file("scripts/deploy.sh", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.SCRIPT
   174→
   175→    def test_bin_directory(self):
   176→        """File in bin/ is classified as SCRIPT."""
   177→        assert classify_file("bin/run.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.SCRIPT
   178→
   179→    def test_production_default(self):
   180→        """File not matching any rule defaults to PRODUCTION."""
   181→        assert classify_file("src/app.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.PRODUCTION
   182→
   183→    def test_production_unknown_path(self):
   184→        """Completely unrecognized path defaults to PRODUCTION."""
   185→        assert (
   186→            classify_file("lib/utils/helpers.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.PRODUCTION
   187→        )
   188→
   189→    def test_first_matching_rule_wins(self):
   190→        """VENDOR comes before TEST in COMMON_ZONE_RULES, so vendor wins."""
   191→        # A file in vendor/tests/ should be VENDOR (first match)
   192→        assert classify_file("vendor/tests/test_x.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES) == Zone.VENDOR
   193→
   194→    def test_override_takes_priority(self):
   195→        """Override value takes priority over rule-based classification."""
   196→        overrides = {"src/app.py": "test"}
   197→        assert classify_file("src/app.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES, overrides) == Zone.TEST
   198→
   199→    def test_override_on_vendor_file(self):
   200→        """Override can reclassify a vendor file as production."""
   201→        overrides = {"vendor/special.py": "production"}
   202→        assert (
   203→            classify_file("vendor/special.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES, overrides)
   204→            == Zone.PRODUCTION
   205→        )
   206→
   207→    def test_override_invalid_zone_falls_through(self):
   208→        """Invalid zone value in override falls through to rule matching."""
   209→        overrides = {"scripts/run.py": "invalid_zone"}
   210→        assert (
   211→            classify_file("scripts/run.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES, overrides) == Zone.SCRIPT
   212→        )
   213→
   214→    def test_override_missing_key_falls_through(self):
   215→        """Override dict present but file not in it falls through to rules."""
   216→        overrides = {"other.py": "test"}
   217→        assert (
   218→            classify_file("src/app.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES, overrides) == Zone.PRODUCTION
   219→        )
   220→
   221→    def test_override_none(self):
   222→        """overrides=None is handled gracefully."""
   223→        assert classify_file("src/app.py", COMMON_ZONE_RULES, None) == Zone.PRODUCTION
   224→
   225→    def test_empty_rules(self):
   226→        """Empty rules list defaults everything to PRODUCTION."""
   227→        assert classify_file("tests/test_foo.py", []) == Zone.PRODUCTION
   228→
   229→    def test_custom_rules(self):
   230→        """Custom rules correctly classify files."""
   231→        rules = [ZoneRule(Zone.CONFIG, ["config.py", ".cfg"])]
   232→        assert classify_file("src/config.py", rules) == Zone.CONFIG
   233→        assert classify_file("setup.cfg", rules) == Zone.CONFIG
   234→        assert classify_file("src/main.py", rules) == Zone.PRODUCTION
   235→

   239→
   240→class TestFileZoneMap:
   241→    """Tests for the FileZoneMap class."""
   242→
   243→    @pytest.fixture
   244→    def sample_files(self):
   245→        """A set of files spanning multiple zones."""
   246→        return [
   247→            "src/app.py",
   248→            "src/utils.py",
   249→            "tests/test_app.py",
   250→            "tests/test_utils.py",
   251→            "scripts/deploy.sh",
   252→            "vendor/lib.py",
   253→            "generated/types.py",
   254→        ]
   255→
   256→    @pytest.fixture
   257→    def zone_map(self, sample_files):
   258→        """FileZoneMap built from sample files and common rules."""
   259→        return FileZoneMap(sample_files, COMMON_ZONE_RULES)
   260→
   261→    def test_get_production_file(self, zone_map):
   262→        """Production file returns Zone.PRODUCTION."""
   263→        assert zone_map.get("src/app.py") == Zone.PRODUCTION
   264→
   265→    def test_get_test_file(self, zone_map):
   266→        """Test file returns Zone.TEST."""
   267→        assert zone_map.get("tests/test_app.py") == Zone.TEST
   268→
   269→    def test_get_script_file(self, zone_map):
   270→        """Script file returns Zone.SCRIPT."""
   271→        assert zone_map.get("scripts/deploy.sh") == Zone.SCRIPT
   272→
   273→    def test_get_vendor_file(self, zone_map):
   274→        """Vendor file returns Zone.VENDOR."""
   275→        assert zone_map.get("vendor/lib.py") == Zone.VENDOR
   276→
   277→    def test_get_generated_file(self, zone_map):
   278→        """Generated file returns Zone.GENERATED."""
   279→        assert zone_map.get("generated/types.py") == Zone.GENERATED
   280→
   281→    def test_get_unknown_file_defaults_production(self, zone_map):
   282→        """File not in the map returns PRODUCTION."""
   283→        assert zone_map.get("unknown/file.py") == Zone.PRODUCTION
   284→
   285→    def test_exclude_zones(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   286→        """exclude() removes files in specified zones."""
   287→        result = zone_map.exclude(sample_files, Zone.TEST, Zone.VENDOR)
   288→        assert "tests/test_app.py" not in result
   289→        assert "tests/test_utils.py" not in result
   290→        assert "vendor/lib.py" not in result
   291→        assert "src/app.py" in result
   292→        assert "scripts/deploy.sh" in result
   293→
   294→    def test_exclude_no_zones(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   295→        """exclude() with no zones returns all files."""
   296→        result = zone_map.exclude(sample_files)
   297→        assert len(result) == len(sample_files)
   298→
   299→    def test_include_only_zones(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   300→        """include_only() returns files in specified zones."""
   301→        result = zone_map.include_only(sample_files, Zone.TEST)
   302→        assert len(result) == 2
   303→        assert "tests/test_app.py" in result
   304→        assert "tests/test_utils.py" in result
   305→
   306→    def test_include_only_multiple_zones(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   307→        """include_only() works with multiple zones."""
   308→        result = zone_map.include_only(sample_files, Zone.PRODUCTION, Zone.SCRIPT)
   309→        assert "src/app.py" in result
   310→        assert "src/utils.py" in result
   311→        assert "scripts/deploy.sh" in result
   312→        assert len(result) == 3
   313→
   314→    def test_include_only_empty_result(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   315→        """include_only() with zone having no files returns empty."""
   316→        result = zone_map.include_only(sample_files, Zone.CONFIG)
   317→        assert result == []
   318→
   319→    def test_counts(self, zone_map):
   320→        """counts() returns correct per-zone tallies."""
   321→        c = zone_map.counts()
   322→        assert c["production"] == 2
   323→        assert c["test"] == 2
   324→        assert c["script"] == 1
   325→        assert c["vendor"] == 1
   326→        assert c["generated"] == 1
   327→
   328→    def test_production_count(self, zone_map):
   329→        """production_count() returns total minus non-production (SCRIPT is not excluded)."""
   330→        # 7 files - 4 non-production (2 test + 1 vendor + 1 generated) = 3
   331→        # (SCRIPT is NOT in EXCLUDED_ZONES so it counts toward production_count)
   332→        assert zone_map.production_count() == 3
   333→
   334→    def test_non_production_count(self, zone_map):
   335→        """non_production_count() returns count of excluded-zone files."""
   336→        # test(2) + vendor(1) + generated(1) = 4 (script is NOT in EXCLUDED_ZONES)
   337→        assert zone_map.non_production_count() == 4
   338→
   339→    def test_all_files(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   340→        """all_files() returns the full file list."""
   341→        result = zone_map.all_files()
   342→        assert set(result) == set(sample_files)
   343→
   344→    def test_items(self, zone_map, sample_files):
   345→        """items() returns (path, zone) tuples for all files."""
   346→        result = zone_map.items()
   347→        assert len(result) == len(sample_files)
   348→        result_dict = dict(result)
   349→        assert result_dict["src/app.py"] == Zone.PRODUCTION
   350→        assert result_dict["tests/test_app.py"] == Zone.TEST
   351→
   352→    def test_rel_fn(self):
   353→        """rel_fn is used to convert paths before classification."""
   354→        files = ["/project/tests/test_foo.py", "/project/src/main.py"]
   355→
   356→        def strip_prefix(p):
   357→            return p.replace("/project/", "")
   358→
   359→        zm = FileZoneMap(files, COMMON_ZONE_RULES, rel_fn=strip_prefix)
   360→        assert zm.get("/project/tests/test_foo.py") == Zone.TEST
   361→        assert zm.get("/project/src/main.py") == Zone.PRODUCTION
   362→        assert zm.get("tests/test_foo.py") == Zone.TEST
   363→        assert zm.get("src/main.py") == Zone.PRODUCTION
   364→
   365→    def test_overrides(self):
   366→        """Overrides are passed through to classify_file."""
   367→        files = ["src/special.py"]
   368→        overrides = {"src/special.py": "test"}
   369→        zm = FileZoneMap(files, COMMON_ZONE_RULES, overrides=overrides)
   370→        assert zm.get("src/special.py") == Zone.TEST
   371→
   372→    def test_empty_file_list(self):
   373→        """FileZoneMap with no files works correctly."""
   374→        zm = FileZoneMap([], COMMON_ZONE_RULES)
   375→        assert zm.all_files() == []
   376→        assert zm.counts() == {}
   377→        assert zm.production_count() == 0
   378→        assert zm.non_production_count() == 0
   379→
   380→    def test_exclude_with_unknown_files(self, zone_map):
   381→        """exclude() handles files not in the map (defaulting to PRODUCTION)."""
   382→        files = ["src/app.py", "unknown.py"]
   383→        result = zone_map.exclude(files, Zone.PRODUCTION)
   384→        # Both are PRODUCTION (one explicitly, one by default), so both excluded
   385→        assert result == []
   386→
   387→    def test_include_only_with_unknown_files(self, zone_map):
   388→        """include_only() handles files not in the map (defaulting to PRODUCTION)."""
   389→        files = ["src/app.py", "unknown.py"]
   390→        result = zone_map.include_only(files, Zone.PRODUCTION)
   391→        assert len(result) == 2
   392→

   397→class TestZoneEnumAndConstants:
   398→    """Tests for Zone enum and related constants."""
   399→
   400→    def test_zone_values(self):
   401→        """All expected zone values exist."""
   402→        assert Zone.PRODUCTION.value == "production"
   403→        assert Zone.TEST.value == "test"
   404→        assert Zone.CONFIG.value == "config"
   405→        assert Zone.GENERATED.value == "generated"
   406→        assert Zone.SCRIPT.value == "script"
   407→        assert Zone.VENDOR.value == "vendor"
   408→
   409→    def test_zone_is_str(self):
   410→        """Zone is a str enum."""
   411→        assert isinstance(Zone.PRODUCTION, str)
   412→
   413→    def test_excluded_zones(self):
   414→        """EXCLUDED_ZONES contains the correct zones."""
   415→        assert EXCLUDED_ZONES == {Zone.TEST, Zone.CONFIG, Zone.GENERATED, Zone.VENDOR}
   416→        # SCRIPT and PRODUCTION are NOT excluded
   417→        assert Zone.SCRIPT not in EXCLUDED_ZONES
   418→        assert Zone.PRODUCTION not in EXCLUDED_ZONES
   419→
   420→    def test_excluded_zone_values(self):
   421→        """EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES contains string values of excluded zones."""
   422→        assert EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES == {"test", "config", "generated", "vendor"}
   423→
   424→    def test_review_exclusion_sets_are_enum_backed(self):
   425→        assert REVIEW_SELECTION_EXCLUDED_ZONES == {
   426→            Zone.TEST,
   427→            Zone.GENERATED,
   428→            Zone.VENDOR,
   429→        }
   430→        assert REVIEW_COVERAGE_EXCLUDED_ZONES == {
   431→            Zone.TEST,
   432→            Zone.GENERATED,
   433→            Zone.VENDOR,
   434→            Zone.CONFIG,
   435→            Zone.SCRIPT,
   436→        }
   437→
   438→    def test_normalize_zone_supports_enum_and_value_objects(self):
   439→        class _ZoneLike:
   440→            def __init__(self, value):
   441→                self.value = value
   442→
   443→        assert normalize_zone(Zone.TEST) == Zone.TEST
   444→        assert normalize_zone("vendor") == Zone.VENDOR
   445→        assert normalize_zone(_ZoneLike("generated")) == Zone.GENERATED
   446→        assert normalize_zone("unknown-zone") is None
   447→
   448→    def test_zone_in_works_for_duck_typed_zone_values(self):
   449→        class _ZoneLike:
   450→            def __init__(self, value):
   451→                self.value = value
   452→
   453→        assert zone_in(_ZoneLike("test"), REVIEW_SELECTION_EXCLUDED_ZONES)
   454→        assert not zone_in(_ZoneLike("config"), REVIEW_SELECTION_EXCLUDED_ZONES)
   455→

   457→# ── ZONE_POLICIES ────────────────────────────────────────────
```
