```
   459→
   460→class TestZonePolicies:
   461→    """Tests for zone policy definitions."""
   462→
   463→    def test_production_policy_is_permissive(self):
   464→        """PRODUCTION policy has no skips or downgrades."""
   465→        policy = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.PRODUCTION]
   466→        assert policy.skip_detectors == set()
   467→        assert policy.downgrade_detectors == set()
   468→        assert policy.exclude_from_score is False
   469→
   470→    def test_test_zone_skips(self):
   471→        """TEST zone skips expected detectors."""
   472→        policy = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.TEST]
   473→        expected_skips = {
   474→            "boilerplate_duplication",
   475→            "dupes",
   476→            "single_use",
   477→            "orphaned",
   478→            "coupling",
   479→            "facade",
   480→            "dict_keys",
   481→            "test_coverage",
   482→            "security",
   483→            "private_imports",
   484→        }
   485→        assert policy.skip_detectors == expected_skips
   486→
   487→    def test_test_zone_downgrades(self):
   488→        """TEST zone downgrades smells and structural."""
   489→        policy = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.TEST]
   490→        assert policy.downgrade_detectors == {"smells", "structural"}
   491→
   492→    def test_test_zone_excluded_from_score(self):
   493→        """TEST zone is excluded from scoring."""
   494→        assert ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.TEST].exclude_from_score is True
   495→
   496→    def test_config_zone_skips(self):
   497→        """CONFIG zone skips a broad set of detectors."""
   498→        policy = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.CONFIG]
   499→        expected_skips = {
   500→            "boilerplate_duplication",
   501→            "smells",
   502→            "structural",
   503→            "dupes",
   504→            "naming",
   505→            "single_use",
   506→            "orphaned",
   507→            "coupling",
   508→            "facade",
   509→            "dict_keys",
   510→            "test_coverage",
   511→            "security",
   512→        }
   513→        assert policy.skip_detectors == expected_skips
   514→
   515→    def test_config_zone_excluded_from_score(self):
   516→        """CONFIG zone is excluded from scoring."""
   517→        assert ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.CONFIG].exclude_from_score is True
   518→
   519→    def test_generated_zone_skips_most(self):
   520→        """GENERATED zone skips nearly all detectors."""
   521→        policy = ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.GENERATED]
   522→        assert "test_coverage" in policy.skip_detectors
   523→        assert "unused" in policy.skip_detectors
   524→        assert "smells" in policy.skip_detectors
   525→        assert "react" in policy.skip_detectors
   526→        assert "naming" in policy.skip_detectors
   527→        assert "cycles" in policy.skip_detectors
   528→
   529→    def test_generated_zone_excluded_from_score(self):
   530→        """GENERATED zone is excluded from scoring."""
   531→        assert ZONE_POLICIES[Zone.GENERATED].exclude_from_score is True
   532→
   533→    def test_vendor_zone_matches_generated(self):
   534→        """VENDOR zone has the same skip set as GENERATED."""
```

```
"""Plan rendering/query helpers (read-only ownership).

This package owns human-readable planning views (markdown/selection helpers)
and does not own plan persistence or queue mutation.

Ownership contract:
- ``engine.plan_state``: persisted plan state/schema/policy surface.
- ``engine.plan_ops``: queue/cluster/skip/annotation mutations.
- ``engine.plan_queue``: reconcile/sync lifecycle orchestration.
- ``engine.plan_triage``: triage prompts/commands/contracts.
- ``engine.planning`` (this package): read-only rendering/query helpers only.

``engine._plan`` remains internal implementation detail.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from pathlib import Path

    from desloppify.engine.planning.scan import PlanScanOptions
    from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanItem, PlanState
    from desloppify.languages.framework import LangConfig, LangRun
    from desloppify.state import Issue


def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
    from desloppify.engine.planning.render import generate_plan_md as _generate_plan_md

    if plan is None:
        return _generate_plan_md(state)
    return _generate_plan_md(state, plan)


def generate_issues(
    path: Path,
    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None = None,
    *,
    options: PlanScanOptions | None = None,
) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    from desloppify.engine.planning.scan import generate_issues as _generate_issues

    if lang is None and options is None:
        return _generate_issues(path)
    if options is None:
        return _generate_issues(path, lang)
    if lang is None:
        return _generate_issues(path, options=options)
    return _generate_issues(path, lang, options=options)


def get_next_item(
    state: PlanState,
    scan_path: str | None = None,
) -> PlanItem | None:
    from desloppify.engine.planning.select import get_next_item as _get_next_item

    if scan_path is None:
        return _get_next_item(state)
    return _get_next_item(state, scan_path=scan_path)


def get_next_items(
    state: PlanState,
    count: int = 1,
    scan_path: str | None = None,
) -> list[PlanItem]:
    from desloppify.engine.planning.select import get_next_items as _get_next_items

    if count == 1 and scan_path is None:
        return _get_next_items(state)
    if scan_path is None:
        return _get_next_items(state, count=count)
    if count == 1:
        return _get_next_items(state, scan_path=scan_path)
    return _get_next_items(state, count=count, scan_path=scan_path)
```

```
1→"""Go function extraction: regex-based with brace-depth tracking.
     2→
     3→Originally contributed by tinker495 (KyuSeok Jung) in PR #128.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→from __future__ import annotations
     7→
     8→import hashlib
     9→import re
    10→from pathlib import Path
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import resolve_path
    13→
    14→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import SourceDiscoveryOptions, find_source_files
    15→from desloppify.engine.detectors.base import FunctionInfo
    16→
    17→GO_FILE_EXCLUSIONS = ["vendor", "testdata", ".git", "node_modules"]
    18→
    19→_FUNC_DECL_RE = re.compile(
    20→    r"(?m)^func\s+"
    21→    r"(?:\(\s*\w+\s+\*?\w+(?:\[[\w,\s]+\])?\s*\)\s*)?"  # optional receiver
    22→    r"(\w+)"  # function name
    23→    r"(?:\[[\w,\s~|]+\])?"  # optional type params (generics)
    24→    r"\s*\("
    25→)
    26→
    27→
    28→def find_go_files(path: Path | str) -> list[str]:
    29→    """Find Go source files under path."""
    30→    return find_source_files(
    31→        path,
    32→        [".go"],
    33→        SourceDiscoveryOptions(exclusions=tuple(GO_FILE_EXCLUSIONS)),
    34→    )
    35→
    36→
    37→def _find_matching_brace(content: str, open_pos: int) -> int | None:
    38→    """Find closing brace for a Go function body with string/comment awareness."""
    39→    depth = 0
    40→    i = open_pos
    41→    length = len(content)
    42→    while i < length:
    43→        ch = content[i]
    44→
    45→        # Backtick raw string — skip until closing backtick
    46→        if ch == '`':
    47→            i += 1
    48→            while i < length and content[i] != '`':
    49→                i += 1
    50→            i += 1
```

```
    33→    tasks: dict[int, BatchTask],
    34→    options: BatchExecutionOptions | None = None,
    35→    progress_fn=None,
    36→    error_log_fn=None,
    37→) -> list[int]:
    38→    """Run indexed tasks and return failed index list.
    39→
    40→    Each value in *tasks* is a zero-arg callable returning an int exit code.
    41→    All domain knowledge (files, prompts, etc.) is pre-bound by the caller.
    42→    """
    43→    resolved_options = _coerce_batch_execution_options(options)
    44→    contract_cache: dict[int, str] = {}
    45→    indexes = sorted(tasks)
    46→    if resolved_options.run_parallel:
    47→        max_workers, heartbeat = _resolve_parallel_runtime(
    48→            indexes=indexes,
    49→            max_parallel_workers=resolved_options.max_parallel_workers,
    50→            heartbeat_seconds=resolved_options.heartbeat_seconds,
    51→        )
    52→        failures: set[int] = set()
    53→        progress_failures: set[int] = set()
    54→        started_at: dict[int, float] = {}
    55→        lock = threading.Lock()
    56→        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as executor:
    57→            futures = _queue_parallel_tasks(
    58→                executor=executor,
    59→                indexes=indexes,
    60→                tasks=tasks,
    61→                progress_fn=progress_fn,
    62→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    63→                contract_cache=contract_cache,
    64→                max_workers=max_workers,
    65→                failures=failures,
    66→                progress_failures=progress_failures,
    67→                started_at=started_at,
    68→                lock=lock,
    69→                clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    70→            )
    71→            pending = set(futures.keys())
    72→            _drain_parallel_completions(
    73→                pending=pending,
    74→                futures=futures,
    75→                heartbeat=heartbeat,
    76→                indexes=indexes,
    77→                progress_fn=progress_fn,
    78→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    79→                contract_cache=contract_cache,
    80→                failures=failures,
    81→                progress_failures=progress_failures,
    82→                started_at=started_at,
    83→                lock=lock,
    84→                clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    85→            )
    86→        return sorted(failures)
    87→    return _execute_serial(
    88→        tasks=tasks,
    89→        indexes=indexes,
    90→        progress_fn=progress_fn,
    91→        error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    92→        clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    93→        contract_cache=contract_cache,
    94→    )
    95→
    96→
    97→def collect_batch_results(
    98→    *,
    99→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   100→    failures: list[int],
   101→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   102→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   103→    extract_payload_fn,
   104→    normalize_result_fn,
   105→) -> tuple[list[BatchResult], list[int]]:
   106→    """Parse and normalize batch outputs, preserving prior failures."""
   107→    batch_results: list[BatchResult] = []
   108→    failure_set = set(failures)
   109→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   110→        had_execution_failure = idx in failure_set
   111→        raw_path = output_files[idx]
   112→        payload = None
   113→        parsed_from_log = False
   114→        if raw_path.exists():
   115→            try:
   116→                payload = extract_payload_fn(raw_path.read_text())
   117→            except OSError as exc:
   118→                logger.warning("Failed reading batch payload %s: %s", raw_path, exc)
   119→                payload = None
   120→        if payload is None:
   121→            payload = extract_payload_from_log(idx, raw_path, extract_payload_fn)
   122→            parsed_from_log = payload is not None
   123→        if payload is None:
   124→            failure_set.add(idx)
   125→            continue
   126→        if parsed_from_log:
   127→            try:
   128→                safe_write_text(raw_path, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   129→            except OSError as exc:
   130→                logger.warning("Failed writing normalized batch payload %s: %s", raw_path, exc)
   131→        try:
   132→            (
   133→                assessments,
   134→                issues,
   135→                dimension_notes,
   136→                dimension_judgment,
   137→                quality,
   138→            ) = normalize_result_fn(payload, allowed_dims)
   139→        except ValueError as exc:
   140→            logger.debug("Invalid batch payload at index %s (%s): %s", idx, raw_path, exc)
   141→            failure_set.add(idx)
   142→            continue
   143→        if had_execution_failure:
   144→            failure_set.discard(idx)
   145→            logger.info(
   146→                "Batch #%d recovered: execution exited non-zero but raw payload parsed successfully",
   147→                idx + 1,
   148→            )
   149→        batch_results.append(
   150→            BatchResult(
   151→                batch_index=idx + 1,
   152→                assessments=assessments,
   153→                dimension_notes=dimension_notes,
   154→                dimension_judgment=dimension_judgment,
   155→                issues=issues,
   156→                quality=quality,
   157→            )
   158→        )
   159→    return batch_results, sorted(failure_set)
   160→
   161→
   162→__all__ = [
   163→    "BatchResult",
   164→    "BatchExecutionOptions",
   165→    "BatchProgressEvent",
   166→    "collect_batch_results",
   167→    "execute_batches",
   168→]
   169→
```

```
1→"""Packet sanitization, hashing, and artifact layout helpers for review batches."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from copy import deepcopy
     7→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     8→from hashlib import sha256
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import Any
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PacketValidationError
    13→
    14→_BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS = {
    15→    "narrative",
    16→    "next_command",
    17→    "score_snapshot",
    18→    "strict_target",
    19→    "strict_target_progress",
    20→    "subjective_at_target",
    21→}
    22→
    23→_BLIND_CONFIG_SCORE_HINT_KEYS = {
    24→    "target_strict_score",
    25→    "strict_target_score",
    26→    "target_score",
    27→    "strict_score",
    28→    "objective_score",
    29→    "overall_score",
    30→    "verified_strict_score",
    31→}
    32→
    33→
    34→def write_packet_snapshot(
    35→    packet: dict[str, Any],
    36→    *,
    37→    stamp: str,
    38→    review_packet_dir: Path,
    39→    blind_path: Path,
    40→    safe_write_text_fn,
    41→) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    42→    """Persist immutable and blind packet snapshots for runner workflows."""
    43→    review_packet_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    44→    packet_path = review_packet_dir / f"holistic_packet_{stamp}.json"
    45→    safe_write_text_fn(packet_path, json.dumps(packet, indent=2) + "\n")
    46→    blind_packet = _build_blind_packet(packet)
    47→    safe_write_text_fn(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
    48→    return packet_path, blind_path
    49→
    50→
    51→def _build_blind_packet(packet: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    52→    """Return a blind-review packet with score anchoring metadata removed."""
    53→    blind = deepcopy(packet)
    54→    for key in _BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS:
    55→        blind.pop(key, None)
    56→
    57→    config = blind.get("config")
    58→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    59→        sanitized = _sanitize_blind_config(config)
    60→        if sanitized:
    61→            blind["config"] = sanitized
    62→        else:
    63→            blind.pop("config", None)
    64→    return blind
    65→
    66→
    67→def build_blind_packet(packet: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    68→    """Public wrapper for blind packet sanitization."""
    69→    return _build_blind_packet(packet)
    70→
    71→
    72→def run_stamp() -> str:
    73→    """Stable UTC run stamp for artifact paths."""
    74→    return datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    75→
    76→
    77→def _sanitize_blind_config(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    78→    """Drop score/target hints from config while preserving unrelated options."""
    79→    sanitized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    80→    for key, value in config.items():
    81→        lowered = key.strip().lower()
    82→        if not lowered:
    83→            continue
    84→        if lowered in _BLIND_CONFIG_SCORE_HINT_KEYS:
    85→            continue
    86→        if "target" in lowered:
    87→            continue
    88→        if lowered.endswith("_score"):
    89→            continue
    90→        sanitized[key] = value
    91→    return sanitized
    92→
    93→
    94→def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    95→    """Compute sha256 hex digest for path contents (or None on read failure)."""
    96→    try:
    97→        data = path.read_bytes()
    98→    except OSError:
    99→        return None
   100→    return sha256(data).hexdigest()
   101→
   102→
   103→def build_batch_import_provenance(
   104→    *,
   105→    runner: str,
   106→    blind_packet_path: Path,
   107→    run_stamp: str,
   108→    batch_indexes: list[int],
   109→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   110→    """Build provenance payload used to trust assessment-bearing imports."""
   111→    packet_hash = sha256_file(blind_packet_path)
   112→    batch_indexes_1 = sorted({int(index) + 1 for index in batch_indexes})
   113→    return {
   114→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   115→        "blind": True,
   116→        "runner": runner,
   117→        "run_stamp": run_stamp,
   118→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   119→        "batch_count": len(batch_indexes_1),
   120→        "batch_indexes": batch_indexes_1,
   121→        "packet_path": str(blind_packet_path),
   122→        "packet_sha256": packet_hash,
   123→    }
   124→
   125→
   126→def selected_batch_indexes(
   127→    *,
   128→    raw_selection: str | None,
   129→    batch_count: int,
   130→    parse_fn,
   131→    colorize_fn,
   132→) -> list[int]:
   133→    """Validate selected batch indexes or raise CommandError."""
   134→    try:
   135→        selected = parse_fn(raw_selection, batch_count)
   136→    except ValueError as exc:
   137→        raise PacketValidationError(str(exc), exit_code=2) from exc
   138→    if selected:
   139→        return selected
   140→    raise PacketValidationError("no batches selected", exit_code=2)
   141→
   142→
   143→def prepare_run_artifacts(
   144→    *,
   145→    stamp: str,
   146→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   147→    batches: list[dict[str, Any]],
   148→    packet_path: Path,
   149→    run_root: Path,
   150→    repo_root: Path,
   151→    build_prompt_fn,
   152→    safe_write_text_fn,
   153→    colorize_fn,
   154→) -> tuple[Path, Path, dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path]]:
   155→    """Build prompt/output/log paths and persist prompts for selected batches."""
   156→    run_dir = run_root / stamp
   157→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   158→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   159→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   160→    prompts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   161→    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   162→    logs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   163→
   164→    selected_1_based = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]
   165→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Running holistic batches: {selected_1_based}", "bold"))
   166→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Run artifacts: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   167→
   168→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   169→    output_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   170→    log_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   171→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   172→        batch = batches[idx] if isinstance(batches[idx], dict) else {}
   173→        prompt_text = build_prompt_fn(
   174→            repo_root=repo_root,
   175→            packet_path=packet_path,
   176→            batch_index=idx,
   177→            batch=batch,
   178→        )
   179→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.md"
   180→        output_file = results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   181→        log_file = logs_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.log"
   182→        safe_write_text_fn(prompt_file, prompt_text)
   183→        prompt_files[idx] = prompt_file
   184→        output_files[idx] = output_file
   185→        log_files[idx] = log_file
   186→    return run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files
   187→
   188→
   189→__all__ = [
   190→    "build_batch_import_provenance",
   191→    "build_blind_packet",
   192→    "prepare_run_artifacts",
   193→    "run_stamp",
   194→    "selected_batch_indexes",
   195→    "sha256_file",
   196→    "write_packet_snapshot",
   197→]
   198→
```

```
    30→class ReviewPacketContext:

    32→
    33→    path: Path

    40→def resolve_review_packet_context(args: Any) -> ReviewPacketContext:
    41→    """Parse shared packet options from CLI args."""
    42→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
    43→    dimensions = list(dims) if dims else None
    44→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
    45→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
    46→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
    47→        default=30,
    48→        minimum=1,
    49→    )
    50→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
    51→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
    52→        default=20,
    53→        minimum=1,
    54→    )
    55→    return ReviewPacketContext(
    56→        path=Path(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
    57→        dimensions=dimensions,
    58→        retrospective=retrospective,
    59→        retrospective_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    60→        retrospective_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
    61→    )
    62→
    63→
    64→def build_holistic_packet(
    65→    *,
    66→    state: StateModel,
    67→    lang: Any,
    68→    config: dict[str, Any],
    69→    context: ReviewPacketContext,
    70→    setup_lang_fn,
    71→    prepare_holistic_review_fn=None,
    72→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str]:
    73→    """Build the canonical holistic review packet payload and lang name."""
    74→    lang_run, found_files = setup_lang_fn(lang, context.path, config)
    75→    lang_name = lang_run.name
    76→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    77→        state,
    78→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
    79→    )
    80→    prepare_fn = prepare_holistic_review_fn or prepare_holistic_review
    81→    packet = prepare_fn(
    82→        context.path,
    83→        lang_run,
    84→        state,
    85→        options=HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
    86→            dimensions=context.dimensions,
    87→            files=found_files or None,
    88→            max_files_per_batch=coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
    89→            include_issue_history=context.retrospective,
    90→            issue_history_max_issues=context.retrospective_max_issues,
    91→            issue_history_max_batch_items=context.retrospective_max_batch_items,
    92→        ),
    93→    )
    94→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
    95→    return packet, lang_name
    96→
    97→
    98→def build_run_batches_next_command(context: ReviewPacketContext) -> str:
    99→    """Return the canonical next command for local batch-based review."""
   100→    parts: list[str] = [
   101→        "desloppify",
   102→        "review",
   103→        "--run-batches",
   104→        "--runner",
   105→        "codex",
   106→        "--parallel",
   107→        "--scan-after-import",
   108→    ]
   109→    if context.dimensions:
   110→        parts.extend(["--dimensions", ",".join(context.dimensions)])
   111→    if context.retrospective:
   112→        parts.extend(
   113→            [
   114→                "--retrospective",
   115→                "--retrospective-max-issues",
   116→                str(context.retrospective_max_issues),
   117→                "--retrospective-max-batch-items",
   118→                str(context.retrospective_max_batch_items),
   119→            ]
   120→        )
   121→    return " ".join(parts)
   122→
   123→
   124→def prepared_packet_contract(
   125→    context: ReviewPacketContext,
   126→    *,
   127→    config: dict[str, Any] | None,
   128→) -> dict[str, object]:
   129→    """Build normalized invocation contract for prepared packet reuse."""
   130→    redacted = redacted_review_config(config or {})
   131→    payload = json.dumps(redacted, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
   132→    return {
   133→        "path": str(context.path.resolve()),
   134→        "dimensions": sorted(context.dimensions or []),
   135→        "retrospective": context.retrospective,
   136→        "retrospective_max_issues": context.retrospective_max_issues,
   137→        "retrospective_max_batch_items": context.retrospective_max_batch_items,
   138→        "config_hash": hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
   139→    }
   140→
   141→
   142→def build_external_submit_next_command(context: ReviewPacketContext) -> str:
   143→    """Return the canonical next command for external-session submit."""
   144→    parts: list[str] = [
   145→        "desloppify",
   146→        "review",
   147→        "--external-submit",
   148→        "--session-id",
   149→        "<id>",
   150→        "--import",
   151→        "<file>",
   152→    ]
   153→    if context.retrospective:
   154→        parts.extend(
   155→            [
   156→                "--retrospective",
   157→                "--retrospective-max-issues",
   158→                str(context.retrospective_max_issues),
   159→                "--retrospective-max-batch-items",
   160→                str(context.retrospective_max_batch_items),
   161→            ]
   162→        )
   163→    return " ".join(parts)
   164→
   165→
   166→def require_non_empty_packet(packet: dict[str, Any], *, path: Path) -> int:
   167→    """Return packet total_files, raising ValueError when no reviewable files exist."""
   168→    total = packet.get("total_files", 0)
   169→    if isinstance(total, bool) or not isinstance(total, int):
   170→        raise ValueError(
   171→            f"invalid review packet shape for path '{path}': total_files must be an integer"
   172→        )
   173→    if total <= 0:
   174→        raise ValueError(f"no files found at path '{path}'. Nothing to review.")
   175→    return total
   176→
   177→
   178→def build_review_packet_payload(
   179→    *,
   180→    state: StateModel,
   181→    lang: Any,
   182→    config: dict[str, Any],
   183→    context: ReviewPacketContext,
   184→    next_command: str,
   185→    setup_lang_fn,
   186→    prepare_holistic_review_fn=None,
   187→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   188→    """Build and validate a holistic review packet without persisting artifacts."""
   189→    packet, _lang_name = build_holistic_packet(
   190→        state=state,
   191→        lang=lang,
   192→        config=config,
   193→        context=context,
   194→        setup_lang_fn=setup_lang_fn,
   195→        prepare_holistic_review_fn=prepare_holistic_review_fn,
   196→    )
   197→    packet["config"] = redacted_review_config(config)
   198→    packet["next_command"] = next_command
   199→    require_non_empty_packet(packet, path=context.path)
   200→    return packet
```

```
1→"""zone command: show/set/clear zone classifications."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.command_runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    12→from desloppify.base import config as config_mod
    13→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
    14→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import FileZoneMap, Zone
    17→from desloppify.state_io import load_state, save_state
    18→
    19→
    20→def cmd_zone(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    21→    """Handle zone subcommands: show, set, clear."""
    22→    action = getattr(args, "zone_action", None)
    23→    if action in (None, "show"):
    24→        _zone_show(args)
    25→    elif action == "set":
    26→        _zone_set(args)
    27→    elif action == "clear":
    28→        _zone_clear(args)
    29→    else:
    30→        raise CommandError("Usage: desloppify zone {show|set|clear}")
    31→
    32→
    33→def _zone_show(args: argparse.Namespace):
    34→    """Show zone classifications for all scanned files."""
    35→    state_file = state_path(args)
    36→    if not state_file.exists():
    37→        raise CommandError("No state file found — run a scan first.")
    38→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    39→    if not lang or not lang.file_finder:
    40→        raise CommandError("No language detected — run a scan first.")
    41→
    42→    path = Path(args.path)
    43→    overrides = command_runtime(args).config.get("zone_overrides", {})
    44→
    45→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    46→    zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    47→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=overrides or None
    48→    )
    49→
    50→    # Group files by zone
    51→    by_zone: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    52→    for f in sorted(files, key=lambda f: rel(f)):
    53→        zone = zone_map.get(f)
    54→        by_zone.setdefault(zone.value, []).append(f)
    55→
    56→    total = len(files)
    57→    print(colorize(f"\nZone classifications ({total} files)\n", "bold"))
    58→
    59→    for zone_val in ["production", "test", "config", "generated", "script", "vendor"]:
    60→        zone_files = by_zone.get(zone_val, [])
    61→        if not zone_files:
    62→            continue
    63→        print(colorize(f"  {zone_val} ({len(zone_files)} files)", "bold"))
    64→        for f in zone_files:
    65→            rp = rel(f)
    66→            is_override = rp in overrides
    67→            suffix = colorize(" (override)", "cyan") if is_override else ""
    68→            print(f"    {rp}{suffix}")
    69→        print()
    70→
    71→    if overrides:
    72→        print(colorize(f"  {len(overrides)} override(s) active", "dim"))
    73→    print(colorize("  Override: desloppify zone set <file> <zone>", "dim"))
    74→    print(colorize("  Clear:    desloppify zone clear <file>", "dim"))
    75→    print_agent_plan(
    76→        ["Fix misclassified files, then re-scan."],
    77→        next_command="desloppify scan",
    78→    )
    79→
    80→
    81→def _zone_set(args: argparse.Namespace):
    82→    """Set a zone override for a file."""
    83→    filepath = args.zone_path
    84→    zone_value = args.zone_value
    85→
    86→    # Validate zone value
    87→    valid_zones = {z.value for z in Zone}
    88→    if zone_value not in valid_zones:
    89→        raise CommandError(
    90→            f"Invalid zone: {zone_value}. Valid: {', '.join(sorted(valid_zones))}"
    91→        )
    92→
    93→    normalized = rel(filepath)
    94→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    95→    config.setdefault("zone_overrides", {})[normalized] = zone_value
    96→    try:
    97→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    98→    except OSError as e:
    99→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {e}") from e
   100→    print(f"  Set {normalized} → {zone_value}")
   101→
   102→    # Apply immediately to state
   103→    try:
   104→        sp = state_path(args)
   105→        if sp.exists():
   106→            state = load_state(sp)
   107→            issues = state.get("issues", {})
   108→            updated = 0
   109→            for issue in issues.values():
   110→                if issue.get("file") == normalized:
   111→                    issue["zone"] = zone_value
   112→                    updated += 1
   113→            if updated:
   114→                save_state(state, sp)
   115→            print(f"  Applied to {updated} issue(s).")
   116→    except (ImportError, OSError, TypeError, ValueError):
   117→        print(colorize("  (Will apply on next scan.)", "dim"))
   118→        return
   119→
   120→
   121→def _zone_clear(args: argparse.Namespace):
   122→    """Clear a zone override for a file."""
   123→    filepath = args.zone_path
   124→
   125→    normalized = rel(filepath)
   126→    config = command_runtime(args).config
   127→    overrides = config.get("zone_overrides", {})
   128→    if normalized in overrides:
   129→        del overrides[normalized]
   130→        try:
   131→            config_mod.save_config(config)
   132→        except OSError as e:
   133→            raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {e}") from e
   134→        print(f"  Cleared override for {normalized}")
   135→
   136→        # Re-stamp matching issues to default zone; next scan will reclassify.
   137→        try:
   138→            sp = state_path(args)
   139→            if sp.exists():
   140→                state = load_state(sp)
   141→                issues = state.get("issues", {})
   142→                updated = 0
   143→                for issue in issues.values():
   144→                    if issue.get("file") == normalized:
   145→                        issue["zone"] = "production"
   146→                        updated += 1
   147→                if updated:
   148→                    save_state(state, sp)
   149→                print(f"  Re-stamped {updated} issue(s) to 'production' (will reclassify on next scan).")
   150→        except (ImportError, OSError, TypeError, ValueError):
   151→            print(colorize("  (Will apply on next scan.)", "dim"))
   152→            return
   153→    else:
   154→        print(colorize(f"  No override found for {normalized}", "yellow"))
   155→
```

```
1→"""Runtime setup helpers for review command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import log
    11→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import FileZoneMap
    12→from desloppify.languages.framework import make_lang_run
    13→
    14→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    15→    from desloppify.languages.framework import LangConfig, LangRun
    16→
    17→
    18→def setup_lang(
    19→    lang: LangConfig,
    20→    path: Path,
    21→    config: dict[str, Any],
    22→    *,
    23→    make_lang_run_fn: Callable[[LangConfig], LangRun],
    24→    file_zone_map_cls: type[FileZoneMap],
    25→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str],
    26→    log_fn: Callable[[str], None],
    27→) -> tuple[LangRun, list[str]]:
    28→    """Build LangRun with zone map + dep graph and return (run, files)."""
    29→    lang_run = make_lang_run_fn(lang)
    30→    files: list[str] = []
    31→
    32→    if lang_run.zone_rules and lang_run.file_finder:
    33→        files = lang_run.file_finder(path)
    34→        zone_overrides = config.get("zone_overrides") or None
    35→        lang_run.zone_map = file_zone_map_cls(
    36→            files, lang_run.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel_fn, overrides=zone_overrides
    37→        )
    38→
    39→    if lang_run.build_dep_graph and lang_run.dep_graph is None:
    40→        try:
    41→            lang_run.dep_graph = lang_run.build_dep_graph(path)
    42→        except (
    43→            OSError,
    44→            UnicodeDecodeError,
    45→            ValueError,
    46→            TypeError,
    47→            RuntimeError,
    48→        ) as exc:
    49→            log_fn(f"  dep graph unavailable for review context: {exc}")
    50→
    51→    return lang_run, files
    52→
    53→
    54→def setup_lang_concrete(lang: LangConfig, path: Path, config: dict) -> tuple[LangRun, list[str]]:
    55→    """Build LangRun with zone map + dep graph using concrete dependencies."""
    56→    return setup_lang(
    57→        lang,
    58→        path,
    59→        config,
    60→        make_lang_run_fn=make_lang_run,
    61→        file_zone_map_cls=FileZoneMap,
    62→        rel_fn=rel,
    63→        log_fn=log,
    64→    )
    65→
    66→
    67→__all__ = ["setup_lang", "setup_lang_concrete"]
    68→
```

```
366→def explode_to_single_dimension(
   367→    batches: list[PromptBatchPayload],
   368→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] | None = None,
   369→) -> list[PromptBatchPayload]:
   370→    """Split multi-dimension batches into one batch per dimension.
   371→
   372→    Preserves seed files and rationale — each exploded batch keeps the same
   373→    file grouping but is scoped to a single dimension. When *dimension_prompts*
   374→    is provided, each exploded batch gets a public ``dimension_prompts`` map
   375→    scoped to its single dimension.
   376→    """
   377→    prompts = dimension_prompts or {}
   378→    result: list[PromptBatchPayload] = []
   379→    for batch in batches:
   380→        dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
   381→        if not isinstance(dims, list):
   382→            result.append(batch)
   383→            continue
   384→        for dim in dims:
   385→            exploded: PromptBatchPayload = {**batch, "dimensions": [dim]}
   386→            dim_prompt = prompts.get(dim)
   387→            if isinstance(dim_prompt, dict):
   388→                exploded["dimension_prompts"] = {str(dim): dim_prompt}
   389→            result.append(exploded)
   390→    return result
   391→
   392→

   394→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...],

   399→        return ""
   400→    lines: list[str] = ["DIMENSION TO EVALUATE:\n"]
   401→    for dim in dimensions:
   402→        prompt = dimension_prompts.get(dim)
   403→        if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
   404→            lines.append(f"## {dim}\n(no rubric available)\n")
   405→            continue
   406→        description = str(prompt.get("description", "")).strip()
   407→        lines.append(f"## {dim}")
   408→        if description:
   409→            lines.append(description)
   410→
   411→        look_for = prompt.get("look_for")
   412→        if isinstance(look_for, list) and look_for:
   413→            lines.append("Look for:")
   414→            for item in look_for:
   415→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   416→
   417→        skip = prompt.get("skip")

   420→            for item in skip:
   421→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   422→        lines.append("")
   423→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
```

```
   110→def normalize_go_body(body: str) -> str:
   111→    """Strip comments, blank lines, and logging for duplicate detection."""
```

```
     5→import logging
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.languages import framework as lang_api
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.base.discovery.source import (
    11→    DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS,
    12→    read_file_text,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import count_lines
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def audit_excluded_dirs(
    21→    exclusions: tuple[str, ...],
    22→    scanned_files: list[str],
    23→    project_root: Path,
    24→) -> list[dict]:
    25→    """Check if any --exclude directory has zero references from scanned code.
    26→
    27→    Returns issues for directories that appear stale (no file references them).
    28→    """
    29→    if not exclusions:
    30→        return []
    31→
    32→    candidate_dirs = [
    33→        ex_dir
    34→        for ex_dir in exclusions
    35→        if ex_dir not in DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS and (project_root / ex_dir).is_dir()
    36→    ]
    37→    if not candidate_dirs:
    38→        return []
    39→
    40→    # Read each scanned file once and mark any exclusions observed in content.
    41→    unresolved = set(candidate_dirs)
    42→    for filepath in scanned_files:
    43→        if not unresolved:
    44→            break
    45→        abs_path = (
    46→            filepath if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else str(project_root / filepath)
    47→        )
    48→        content = read_file_text(abs_path)
    49→        if content is None:
    50→            logger.debug(
    51→                "Skipping unreadable file %s while auditing exclusions", filepath
    52→            )
    53→            continue
    54→
    55→        matched = {ex_dir for ex_dir in unresolved if ex_dir in content}
    56→        if matched:
    57→            unresolved.difference_update(matched)
    58→
    59→    stale_issues = []
    60→    for ex_dir in candidate_dirs:
    61→        if ex_dir not in unresolved:
    62→            continue
    63→        stale_issues.append(
    64→            state_mod.make_issue(
    65→                "stale_exclude",
    66→                ex_dir,
    67→                ex_dir,
    68→                tier=4,
    69→                confidence="low",
    70→                summary=(
    71→                    f"Excluded directory '{ex_dir}' has 0 references from scanned code — "
    72→                    "may be stale"
    73→                ),
    74→                detail={"directory": ex_dir, "references": 0},
    75→            )
    76→        )
    77→    return stale_issues
    78→
    79→
    80→def collect_codebase_metrics(lang, path: Path) -> dict | None:
```

```
   120→    stamp: str,
   121→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Path, Path, str, list[str], list[str], list[dict[str, Any]], list[int]]:
   122→    packet, immutable_packet_path, prompt_packet_path = deps.load_or_prepare_packet_fn(
   123→        args,
   124→        state=state,
   125→        lang=lang,
   126→        config=config,
   127→        stamp=stamp,
   128→    )
   129→    scan_path = str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   130→    packet_dimensions = normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   131→    scored_dimensions = scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   132→    print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   133→        selected_dims=packet_dimensions,
   134→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   135→        explicit_selection=bool(getattr(args, "dimensions", None)),
   136→        scan_path=scan_path,
   137→        colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
   138→    )
   139→    suggested_prepare_cmd = f"desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}"
   140→    raw_dim_prompts = packet.get("dimension_prompts")
   141→    batches = explode_to_single_dimension(
   142→        require_batches(

   146→        ),
   147→        dimension_prompts=raw_dim_prompts if isinstance(raw_dim_prompts, dict) else None,
   148→    )
   149→    selected_indexes = deps.selected_batch_indexes_fn(args, batch_count=len(batches))

   161→
   162→def _print_runtime_expectation(
   163→    *,
   164→    deps: BatchRunDeps,
   165→    total_batches: int,
   166→    run_parallel: bool,
   167→    max_parallel_batches: int,
   168→    batch_timeout_seconds: float,
   169→) -> None:
   170→    effective_workers = min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches) if run_parallel else 1
   171→    waves = max(1, math.ceil(total_batches / max(1, effective_workers)))
   172→    worst_case_minutes = max(1, math.ceil((waves * batch_timeout_seconds) / 60))
   173→    print(
   174→        deps.colorize_fn(
   175→            "  Runtime expectation: "
   176→            f"{total_batches} batch(es), workers={effective_workers}, "
   177→            f"timeout-per-batch={int(batch_timeout_seconds / 60)}m, "
   178→            f"worst-case upper bound ~{worst_case_minutes}m.",
   179→            "dim",
   180→        )
   181→    )
```

```
1→"""Source-file discovery, exclusions, and scan-scoped content caching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from contextlib import contextmanager
     7→from collections.abc import Iterator
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import matches_exclusion
    12→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import (

    15→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import (
    16→    safe_relpath as _safe_relpath,
    17→)

    19→from desloppify.base.runtime_state import (
    20→    FileTextReadResult,
    21→    RuntimeContext,
    22→    SourceFileCache,
    23→    resolve_runtime_context,
    24→)
    25→
    26→# Directories that are never useful to scan — always pruned during traversal.
    27→DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS = frozenset(
    28→    {
    29→        "node_modules",
    30→        ".git",
    31→        "__pycache__",
    32→        ".venv",
    33→        ".venv*",
    34→        "venv",
    35→        ".env",
    36→        "dist",
    37→        "build",
    38→        ".next",
    39→        ".nuxt",
    40→        ".output",
    41→        ".tox",
    42→        ".mypy_cache",
    43→        ".pytest_cache",
    44→        ".ruff_cache",
    45→        ".eggs",
    46→        "*.egg-info",
    47→        ".svn",
    48→        ".hg",
    49→    }
    50→)
    51→
    52→
    53→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    54→class SourceDiscoveryOptions:
    55→    """Options bundle for source-file discovery and cache lookups."""
    56→
    57→    exclusions: tuple[str, ...] | None = None
    58→    extra_exclusions: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    59→    project_root: Path | None = None
    60→    source_file_cache: SourceFileCache | None = None
    61→
    62→
    63→def set_exclusions(
    64→    patterns: list[str],
    65→    *,
    66→    runtime: RuntimeContext | None = None,
    67→) -> None:
    68→    """Set global exclusion patterns (called once from CLI at startup)."""
    69→    resolved_runtime = resolve_runtime_context(runtime)
    70→    resolved_runtime.exclusions = tuple(patterns)
    71→    resolved_runtime.source_file_cache.clear()
    72→
    73→
    74→def get_exclusions(*, runtime: RuntimeContext | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    75→    """Return current extra exclusion patterns."""
    76→    return resolve_runtime_context(runtime).exclusions
    77→
    78→
    79→def enable_file_cache(*, runtime: RuntimeContext | None = None) -> None:
    80→    """Enable scan-scoped file content cache."""
    81→    resolved_runtime = resolve_runtime_context(runtime)
    82→    resolved_runtime.file_text_cache.enable()
    83→    resolved_runtime.cache_enabled = True
    84→
    85→
    86→def disable_file_cache(*, runtime: RuntimeContext | None = None) -> None:
    87→    """Disable file content cache and free memory."""
    88→    resolved_runtime = resolve_runtime_context(runtime)
    89→    resolved_runtime.file_text_cache.disable()
    90→    resolved_runtime.cache_enabled = False
    91→
    92→
    93→@contextmanager
    94→def file_cache_scope(*, runtime: RuntimeContext | None = None) -> Iterator[None]:
    95→    """Temporarily enable file cache within a context, with nested safety."""
    96→    resolved_runtime = resolve_runtime_context(runtime)
    97→    was_enabled = resolved_runtime.cache_enabled
    98→    if not was_enabled:
    99→        enable_file_cache(runtime=resolved_runtime)
   100→    try:
   101→        yield
   102→    finally:
   103→        if not was_enabled:
   104→            disable_file_cache(runtime=resolved_runtime)
   105→
   106→
   107→def is_file_cache_enabled(*, runtime: RuntimeContext | None = None) -> bool:
   108→    """Return whether scan-scoped file cache is currently enabled."""
   109→    return resolve_runtime_context(runtime).cache_enabled
   110→
   111→
   112→def read_file_text(filepath: str, *, runtime: RuntimeContext | None = None) -> str | None:
   113→    """Read a file as text, with optional caching."""
   114→    return resolve_runtime_context(runtime).file_text_cache.read(filepath)
   115→
   116→
   117→def read_file_text_result(
   118→    filepath: str,
   119→    *,
   120→    runtime: RuntimeContext | None = None,
   121→) -> FileTextReadResult:
   122→    """Read a file as text and include read-status metadata."""
   123→    return resolve_runtime_context(runtime).file_text_cache.read_result(filepath)
   124→
   125→
   126→def clear_source_file_cache_for_tests(*, runtime: RuntimeContext | None = None) -> None:
   127→    resolve_runtime_context(runtime).source_file_cache.clear()
   128→
   129→
   130→def collect_exclude_dirs(
   131→    scan_root: Path,
   132→    *,
   133→    extra_exclusions: tuple[str, ...] | None = None,
   134→    runtime: RuntimeContext | None = None,
   135→) -> list[str]:
   136→    """All exclusion directories as absolute paths, for passing to external tools.
   137→
   138→    Combines DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS (non-glob entries) + get_exclusions() (runtime/config),
   139→    resolves each against *scan_root*. Filters out glob patterns (``*`` in name)
   140→    since most CLI tools want plain directory paths.
   141→    """
   142→    resolved_exclusions = (
   143→        extra_exclusions
   144→        if extra_exclusions is not None
   145→        else get_exclusions(runtime=runtime)
   146→    )
   147→    patterns = set()
   148→    for pat in DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS:
   149→        if "*" not in pat:
   150→            patterns.add(pat)
```

```
+- ``engine.plan_triage``: triage prompts/commands/contracts.
```

```
   121→        failure_set: set[int],
   122→        execution_failure_set: set[int],
   123→    ) -> None:
   124→        for idx in selected_indexes:
   125→            key = str(idx + 1)
   126→            state = self.batch_status.setdefault(
   127→                key,
   128→                {"position": self.batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   129→            )
   130→            if idx not in failure_set:

   132→                continue
   133→            if idx in execution_failure_set:
   134→                state["status"] = "failed"
   135→                continue
   136→            if not self.output_files[idx].exists():

   138→                continue
   139→            state["status"] = "parse_failed"
```

```
   224→        run_summary["interrupted"] = True
   225→        if interruption_reason:
   226→            run_summary["interruption_reason"] = interruption_reason
   227→    safe_write_text_fn(summary_path, json.dumps(run_summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   228→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Run summary: {summary_path}", "dim"))
   229→    append_run_log_fn(f"run-summary {summary_path}")
   230→
   231→
   232→def _run_batch_task(
   233→    *,
   234→    batch_index: int,
   235→    prompt_path: Path,
   236→    output_path: Path,
   237→    log_path: Path,
   238→    project_root: Path,

   240→) -> int:
   241→    try:
   242→        prompt = prompt_path.read_text()
   243→    except OSError as exc:
   244→        raise RuntimeError(
   245→            f"unable to read prompt for batch #{batch_index + 1}: {prompt_path}"
   246→        ) from exc
   247→    return run_codex_batch_fn(
   248→        prompt=prompt,
   249→        repo_root=project_root,
   250→        output_file=output_path,
   251→        log_file=log_path,
   252→    )
   253→
   254→

   261→    "write_run_summary",
   262→]
```

```
    32→) -> None:
    33→    """Merge concern payload into an existing dimension batch when available."""
```

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive report.

## Summary: Generated File Handling in Desloppify

Based on my investigation of the codebase, here's what already exists and what would need to be added:

### What Already Exists

1. **Zone Classification System** (`desloppify/engine/policy/zones.py`)
   - Desloppify already has a sophisticated zone classification system that automatically excludes generated files
   - Zone enum includes: PRODUCTION, TEST, CONFIG, GENERATED, SCRIPT, VENDOR
   - GENERATED zone is automatically excluded from all detectors and scoring (via ZONE_POLICIES)

2. **Go Language Support**
   - Go is already a fully-supported language plugin at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/go/`
   - Current zone rules defined in `GO_ZONE_RULES` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/go/__init__.py`:
     ```python
     GO_ZONE_RULES = [
         ZoneRule(Zone.TEST, ["_test.go"]),
     ] + COMMON_ZONE_RULES
     ```

3. **Common Zone Rules** (`desloppify/engine/policy/zones.py`, lines 124-129)
   - Already includes directory-based detection:
     ```python
     ZoneRule(Zone.GENERATED, ["/generated/", "/__generated__/"]),
     ```

4. **Pattern Matching Capabilities** (`_match_pattern` function, lines 88-120)
   - Supports multiple pattern types:
     - **Directory patterns**: `/dir/` → substring match on full path (e.g., `/generated/`)
     - **Suffix patterns**: `.ext` → contains on basename (e.g., `.d.ts`, `.generated.go`)
     - **Prefix patterns**: `prefix_` → basename starts-with
     - **Suffix patterns**: `_suffix` → basename ends-with (e.g., `_test.go`)
     - **Exact match**: `name.py` → exact basename match
     - **Fallback**: substring on full path

5. **Language-Specific Zone Rules**
   - Python: `/migrations/`, `_pb2.py`, `_pb2_grpc.py` are marked GENERATED
   - TypeScript: `.d.ts`, `/migrations/` are marked GENERATED
   - These patterns are in language-specific `_zones.py` files

6. **File Content Reading Infrastructure**
   - `read_file_text()` function exists in `desloppify/base/discovery/source.py` (line 112)
   - Used by scan helpers to check file contents
   - Optional file caching system available via `enable_file_cache()`

### What Needs to Be Added

To support files like:
- `// Code generated by github.com/99designs/gqlgen, DO NOT EDIT.`
- Files named `chat.generated.go`

You would need to implement **content-based generation detection**. Currently, desloppify only uses:
1. **Path-based patterns** (directory names, file naming conventions)
2. **Manual overrides** via `zone_overrides` config

### Implementation Options

#### Option 1: Add Go-Specific Zone Rules (Easiest)
Create `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/go/_zones.py` to extend `GO_ZONE_RULES`:

```python
from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import COMMON_ZONE_RULES, Zone, ZoneRule

GO_ZONE_RULES = [
    ZoneRule(Zone.GENERATED, [".generated.", "schema_gen"]),
    ZoneRule(Zone.TEST, ["_test.go"]),
] + COMMON_ZONE_RULES
```

This would handle naming patterns like `query.generated.go` immediately. Test this with:
```bash
python3 -c "
from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import _match_pattern
assert _match_pattern('api/v1/query.generated.go', '.generated.')
"
```

#### Option 2: Add Content-Based Detection (More Comprehensive)
Add a new detection phase or pre-scan hook that:
1. Reads the first 5-10 lines of each Go file
2. Checks for patterns like:
   - `Code generated by`
   - `DO NOT EDIT`
   - `@generated` (from Facebook's Thrift)
   - `autogenerated on` (from protoc)
3. Sets zone overrides in config before scanning

This would require:
- New function in `desloppify/languages/go/` (e.g., `detect_generated_files()`)
- Integration into the scan preflight phase or `GoConfig.__init__`
- Caching via the existing `file_text_cache`

#### Option 3: Hybrid Approach (Recommended)
1. **Immediate**: Add naming patterns to Go zone rules (`.generated.`, common suffixes like `_gen.go`)
2. **Later**: Add optional header detection via a per-language hook

### Current File Paths for Reference

- Zone classification: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/policy/zones.py`
- Go plugin: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/go/__init__.py`
- Python zones: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/python/_zones.py`
- TypeScript zones: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/_zones.py`
- Pattern matching: `_match_pattern()` in zones.py (lines 88-120)
- File reading: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/base/discovery/source.py` (line 112+)

### Zone Policy for Generated Files

Once classified as GENERATED, files automatically receive:
- `skip_detectors = SKIP_ALL_DETECTORS` (26 detectors skipped)
- `exclude_from_score = True` (not counted in health score)
- No review selection or coverage analysis

Perfect! The code is already fixed. Let me create a comprehensive summary of the investigation:

## Summary of Investigation

I've thoroughly investigated the crash you reported and found the **root cause and fix history**:

### The Crash
```
AttributeError: module 'desloppify.engine.planning' has no attribute 'generate_issues'
```

This error occurred when calling `plan_mod.generate_issues()` at what was line 346 in `workflow.py`.

### Root Cause
The issue was introduced in **commit 1085da93** ("desloppify: tighten plan and triage runtime contracts"), which:
1. Removed the `generate_issues()` wrapper function from `/desloppify/engine/planning/__init__.py`
2. Removed it from the `__all__` exports list
3. BUT failed to update the import in `/desloppify/app/commands/scan/workflow.py`

At that point, `workflow.py` still had:
```python
from desloppify.engine import planning as plan_mod
# ... later in code:
issues, potentials = plan_mod.generate_issues(...)  # ERROR: method doesn't exist
```

### The Fix
The issue was **already fixed** in **commit c66eec90** ("desloppify: simplify cluster strategy and fix preflight backlog counting"), which changed the import in `workflow.py` to:
```python
from desloppify.engine.planning.scan import PlanScanOptions, generate_issues as generate_plan_issues
# ... later in code:
issues, potentials = generate_plan_issues(...)  # Correct - direct import from scan module
```

### Current State (Verified)
Your current code at `/desloppify/app/commands/scan/workflow.py` line 53 correctly imports:
```python
from desloppify.engine.planning.scan import PlanScanOptions, generate_issues as generate_plan_issues
```

And line 355 correctly calls:
```python
issues, potentials = generate_plan_issues(...)
```

### Key Files Involved
- `/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/__init__.py` - Exports `generate_plan_md`, `get_next_item`, `get_next_items` (NOT `generate_issues`)
- `/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/scan.py` - Contains the actual `generate_issues()` function (lines 139-156)
- `/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/workflow.py` - Correctly imports and uses it

**The code is working correctly as of the current commit.**

Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the issue. Let me write up the findings:

## Investigation Summary

I've traced through the codebase and identified the root cause of the "missing result files for batches" error when running `desloppify review --import-run`. Here's the complete picture:

### Error Location
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch/orchestrator.py` (lines 474-484)

```python
# -- locate and parse raw batch results --
results_dir = run_dir / "results"
selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in selected]  # convert 1-based to 0-based
output_files = {
    idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
    for idx in selected_indexes
}

missing = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes if not output_files[idx].exists()]
if missing:
    raise CommandError(f"missing result files for batches: {missing}", exit_code=1)
```

### Root Cause: Batch Expansion Mismatch

The issue stems from a **mismatch between how many batches are created during the prepare phase versus how many result files are expected during import**.

**What happens during `--run-batches`:**

1. **Batch Creation** (`prepare_holistic_batches.py`):
   - Raw investigation batches are created by `build_investigation_batches_fn()`
   - These raw batches may have multiple dimensions each

2. **Batch Expansion** (`prompt_sections.py`, line 366-390):
   - The function `explode_to_single_dimension()` is called in `execution_phases.py` (line 141)
   - This **splits multi-dimension batches into one batch per dimension**
   - Example: If a raw batch has 3 dimensions, it becomes 3 separate batches
   - This expansion happens in `_prepare_packet_scope()` at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch/execution_phases.py` (lines 141-148)

3. **Result Files Written**:
   - Result files are named `batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt` where `idx` is 0-based
   - These are written for each **exploded batch** (after dimension expansion)
   - For example, if you have 20 exploded batches, you get `batch-1.raw.txt` through `batch-20.raw.txt`

4. **Run Summary Records Selection**:
   - The `run_summary.json` records `"selected_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in summary_config.selected_indexes]` 
   - This is also in terms of **exploded batch indexes** (after dimension splitting)
   - See `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batches_runtime.py` (line 197)

**What happens during `--import-run`:**

1. **Reading the Summary**:
   - `do_import_run()` reads `run_summary.json` and gets `selected_batches` 
   - This list contains 1-based batch numbers (e.g., `[1, 2, 3, ..., 20]`)

2. **Converting to Indexes**:
   - Line 476: `selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in selected]` → converts to 0-based
   - Creates mapping: `output_files = {idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt" for idx in selected_indexes}`

3. **Looking for Result Files**:
   - The code expects to find files at the **exploded batch indexes**
   - Should match what was actually run

### The Actual Problem

The error occurs when:
1. **Batches are created but exploded during prepare/run** (e.g., 10 raw batches → 20 exploded batches)
2. **The run executes and produces result files** for all 20 exploded batches: `batch-1.raw.txt` through `batch-20.raw.txt`
3. **The `run_summary.json` correctly records** `"selected_batches": [1, 2, ..., 20]` (the exploded batch numbers)
4. **But on `--import-run`, the detection of "missing files" may fail** if:
   - The results directory doesn't exist
   - Files were created with a different naming convention
   - The run was interrupted and some files are actually missing
   - There's a path resolution issue (absolute vs. relative paths)

### Key Code Flows

**During `--run-batches` (normal execution):**
1. `do_run_batches()` → `prepare_batch_run()` 
2. `_prepare_packet_scope()` calls `explode_to_single_dimension()` → expands batches
3. `_prepare_run_runtime()` → `prepare_run_artifacts()` creates prompt/output/log dirs
4. Output files created: `results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"` where `idx` is **0-based exploded index**
5. `run_summary.json` records: `"selected_batches": [1, 2, ..., N]` (1-based exploded indexes)

**During `--import-run`:**
1. `do_import_run()` reads `run_summary.json`
2. Gets `selected_batches` and converts: `selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in selected]` 
3. Tries to find files at: `results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"`
4. **Fails if:**
   - `results_dir` path is wrong
   - Files weren't actually created (run incomplete)
   - Path resolution doesn't match between run phases

### Files Involved in the Issue

1. **Error check location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch/orchestrator.py` (lines 474-484)
2. **Batch expansion:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py` (lines 366-390)
3. **Batch selection in execution:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch/execution_phases.py` (lines 141-148)
4. **Run summary writer:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batches_runtime.py` (lines 179-229, especially line 197)
5. **Batch artifact preparation:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/runner_packets.py` (lines 143-186)

### Debugging Steps

When the error occurs, check:
1. Verify `run_summary.json` exists and contains valid `selected_batches` list
2. Verify the `results_dir` path resolves correctly (usually `{run_dir}/results`)
3. List actual files in `results_dir` to see what was created: `ls {results_dir}/batch-*.raw.txt`
4. Compare the actual files with what `selected_batches` expects
5. Check if the run was fully completed or interrupted mid-execution
